Abstract: In bimanual robotic manipulation, task-relevant visual information varies with the task stage and context, while the interaction of the two arms shifts between independent and coordinated modes, making policy learning challenging. However, existing monolithic Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies process diverse visual inputs and interaction patterns through a single shared representation and action generation pathway, often failing to separately account for visual relevance and bimanual interaction structure. To address this issue, we propose a bimanual manipulation VLA framework based on Dual-Level Structural Decomposition. The View-Selective Visual Router dynamically adjusts wrist-view contributions to emphasize relevant visual cues, while the Interaction-Aware Action Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) decomposes action generation into coordinated and arm-wise pathways to adapt to varying bimanual interaction modes. We evaluate the proposed method on six simulated bimanual manipulation tasks in RoboTwin 2.0 and three long-horizon real-world tasks. Our model improves the overall average success rate over a monolithic baseline by 27.7% in simulation and 43.3% in real-world evaluation, while consistently outperforming single-module variants across both settings. These results demonstrate that jointly considering selective visual processing and explicit decomposition of bimanual interaction structures provides an effective inductive bias for robust bimanual manipulation.

</details>


### [114] [EMG-Based Adaptation of Anisotropic Virtual Fixtures for Robot-Assisted Surgical Resection and Dissection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13340)
*Dario Onfiani,Michael Dyck,Luigi Biagiotti,Julian Klodmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对机器人辅助腹腔镜手术，提出了一种能够根据外科医生肌电信号实时调整的自适应虚拟约束系统，有效提升了手术精度并降低了操作负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人辅助手术中的虚拟约束通常基于静态几何，缺乏对复杂手术流程及医生实时操作意图的自适应能力，限制了在精细手术（如切除和解剖）中的灵活性。

Method: 1. 开发了一种自适应和各向异性的虚拟约束（Virtual Fixture）框架，克服了传统固定几何形状约束的局限性；2. 设计了一种基于肌电信号（EMG）的直观控制接口，实时解析外科医生的操作意图，实现约束强度的动态调制与自由/受限状态的无缝切换。

Result: 试点用户研究表明，与传统方法相比，该系统显著提升了任务完成的准确性和动作一致性，同时降低了医生在手术过程中的认知负荷、努力程度及挫败感。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的自适应各向异性虚拟约束框架及基于肌电信号（EMG）的意图识别交互界面，通过实现手术工具约束的实时动态调节，能够显著提升机器人辅助腹腔镜手术的操作精准度、一致性，并有效降低外科医生的认知负荷及操作压力。

Abstract: In this paper, we address the development of an adaptive assistance system for robot-assisted laparoscopic surgery, specifically for delicate tasks such as Resection and Dissection. Even if Virtual Fixtures offer significant advantages for guiding a surgeon's movements, conventional Virtual Fixtures are often defined by fixed geometries, lacking the flexibility to adapt to the surgical workflow or the surgeon's immediate intent. To address these limitations, we propose a novel framework for an adaptive and anisotropic virtual fixture. In addition, we introduce an intuitive control interface that modulates the fixture's geometry in real-time based on the surgeon's intent, inferred from EMG signals. This approach allows the surgeon to dynamically expand or disengage the constraint by contracting their forearm muscles, enabling seamless transitions between precise guided motion and free repositioning of the tool. Experimental results from a pilot user study, based on a standardized surgical training task, demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method. The system showed significant improvements in task accuracy and movement consistency, alongside a reduction in perceived cognitive load, effort, and frustration.

</details>


### [115] [Low cost, easily manufactured, highly flexible strain and touch sensitive fiber for robotics applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13352)
*Christian Diaz Herrera,Srushti Raste,Simin Liu,Miles Modeste,Jiyang,Yin,Katelyn McCall,Yuxing Jared Yao,Roopkamal Chahal,Simon Chidley,Trung Ha,T. David Westmoreland,Sonia Roberts*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅需极低成本市售材料与工具即可快速制造的导电纤维，该纤维可作为电阻式应变传感器或电容式触碰/近场传感器，适用于多种机器人交互应用且具备可修复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人拉伸和触觉传感器通常在材料、制造设备或时间成本方面较为昂贵。研究旨在寻找一种低成本、易于快速制造且具有实用价值的替代方案。

Method: 通过将导电线穿入硅胶管中形成阻性应变传感器，或将其编织以实现电容式触感传感；并开发了针对性的传感器修复工艺。该制造工艺简单快捷，20厘米纤维仅需约2分钟即可完成。

Result: 成功实现了阻性应变传感（用于气动手指抓取、机器人带位姿感测、柔性体位姿估计）和电容式传感（用于物理接触触发及近场手势跟随）。验证了纤维的耐用性，并提供了一种修复断裂纤维的方法。

Conclusion: 该研究证明，利用廉价、易获取的现成材料和工具可以制造出高性能、多用途的导电纤维传感器，为低成本柔性电子和机器人传感提供了一种低门槛且可修复的解决方案。

Abstract: Existing stretch and touch sensors for robots are generally expensive with respect to at least one of material costs, required manufacturing equipment, or manufacturing time. We present and experimentally characterize a conductive fiber made using only inexpensive commercial off-the-shelf parts (conductive thread at $0.07/ft, silicone tubing at $0.94/ft) and tools (loop-style needle threader at $2), which can be manufactured quickly (20 cm length in 2 minutes.) We demonstrate its use as a resistive strain sensor with three applications: Triggering a grasp in a pneumatically actuated assistive finger, sensing the pose of a pneumatically actuated robotic strap, and estimating the pose of a flexible solid. We also demonstrate that it can be used as a capacitive sensor with two applications: First, as a touch sensor which triggers a commercial robot arm to move, and second, as a near-field sensor enabling the robot arm to follow a moving hand. The capacitive sensors are knitted, showcasing the high flexibility of the fiber. We discuss methods for improving manufacturing scalability and their cost trade-offs. Finally, we demonstrate a method for repairing a cut fiber.

</details>


### [116] [Real-Time Execution with Autoregressive Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13355)
*Sangkyu Lee,Seohyeon Park,Tackgeun You,Avi Caciularu,Idan Szpektor,Hwasup Lim,Youngjae Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过调整Token视野和约束解码使自回归策略实现实时推理的方法，证明了自回归策略在实时任务中比扩散/流匹配策略更具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实时执行研究多集中于扩散策略，忽视了响应速度更慢的自回归策略。为了实现大规模视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在实际场景中的部署，亟需解决自回归策略在推理延迟上的瓶颈。

Method: 提出了针对自回归策略的实时执行方案，主要包含：调整Token化视野（tokenization horizon）以及应用约束解码（constrained decoding），以确保严格的延迟界限并支持多轨迹解码。

Result: 在模拟和真实环境中，该方法实现了实时执行。自回归策略在任务完成速度上显著优于同步推理，且整体表现优于同等规模的流匹配策略。

Conclusion: 通过调整Token量化视野和应用约束解码，自回归策略能够实现实时执行，且在任务完成速度、性能表现、收敛速度和指令遵循的泛化能力方面均优于同等规模的流匹配（flow-matching）策略。

Abstract: Real-time execution, enabled by asynchronous inference that ensures both smooth action trajectories and fast reactivity, is critical for realistic deployments of large-scale Vision-Language-Action models. However, recent work on real-time execution primarily focuses on variants of diffusion policies, even though it is more critical for autoregressive policies given their slower rollout speed in synchronous inference. In contrast, we demonstrate that autoregressive policies can achieve real-time execution by adjusting the tokenization horizon and applying constrained decoding, thereby guaranteeing strict latency bounds that enable multi-trajectory decoding to maximize performance. Across simulated and real-world environments, we find that the autoregressive policy consistently outperforms its equivalent-level flow-matching policy counterpart while achieving significantly improved task completion speeds from synchronous inference. Coupled with the inherent advantages of autoregressive policies, such as faster convergence and better generalizability in instruction-following, these results confirm that autoregressive policies can remain a competitive policy type supporting real-time execution.

</details>


### [117] [GeoHAT: Geometry-Adaptive Hybrid Action Transformer for Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13394)
*Xiangyu Zhu,Renjun Wu,Luzhou Ge,Jinyan Liu,Xuesong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对全身移动操作，GeoHAT 提出一种轻量级几何深度注入策略与解耦的任务专用动作解码方案，在提升空间感知能力的同时显著提高了操作任务的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身移动操作中现有的策略在几何感知上缺乏密集的空间结构，且在统一动作维度下无法兼顾手臂与底盘不同的控制需求；此外，传统的密集融合方式易受噪声深度干扰且计算开销较大。

Method: 1. 几何感知：使用轻量级 Fourier 空间编码器提取像素级 3D 坐标作为几何 token，替代沉重的 3D 主干网络。2. 空间融合：引入基于深度有效性调制的“门控融合”策略，选择性地将几何信息注入视觉基座模型（VFM）特征，避免破坏预训练表示。3. 动作生成：提出“混合全身动作解码器”，将手臂和基座动作在不同子空间进行解耦，并利用稀疏交叉注意力机制处理各模态的任务相关上下文，结合因果时序建模捕捉动作关联性。

Result: 在 ManiSkill-HAB 仿真基准上达到 79.3% 的平均成功率，比最优基准高出 23.7%；并在真实世界任务中验证了优越性。

Conclusion: GeoHAT 通过轻量级几何注入与解耦的动作生成机制，有效解决了全身移动操作中几何感知与动作控制的挑战，在仿真和真实环境中均表现出显著的性能优势。

Abstract: Whole-body mobile manipulation requires coordinating mobile base and manipulator under shifting viewpoints, posing challenges in geometric perception and action generation. Current policies either rely on 2D features or sparse 3D representations that lack dense spatial structure, and typically encode arm and base within one action vector that ignores their distinct control demands. Moreover, existing dense fusion strategies risk corrupting pretrained representations under noisy depth while incurring heavy computational overhead. We present GeoHAT, an end-to-end diffusion-based framework built on a simple principle: geometry should be injected only where reliable and attended to only where needed. GeoHAT employs a lightweight Fourier spatial encoder that maps dense per-pixel 3D coordinates into geometric tokens without an additional 3D vision backbone. These tokens are then selectively injected into vision foundation model features through per-token gated fusion modulated by depth validity, preserving the semantic prior while enriching spatial understanding. For action generation, a Hybrid Whole-Body Action Decoder decomposes arm and base into distinct subspaces and lets each action modality attend to its task-relevant visual context through sparse cross-attention, while causal temporal modeling captures intra-timestep coordination and inter-timestep dependencies. Experiments on the ManiSkill-HAB simulation benchmark demonstrate that GeoHAT achieves a 79.3% mean success rate, surpassing the strongest baseline by 23.7%. Furthermore, real-world experiments on diverse tasks also confirm consistent improvements over all baselines.

</details>


### [118] [GIVE: Grounding Human Gestures in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13435)
*Pengfei Liu,Gen Li,Junqiao Fan,Boyu Ma,Jindou Jia,Yang Xiao,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 为解决机器人仅依靠文本指导导致的交互局限性，GIVE 框架通过视觉特征叠加与语义解释增强 VLA 模型，显著提升了机器人对人类手势信息的理解及任务执行成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型主要基于文本处理任务，忽略了人类交流中手势等非语言线索的重要性，导致在模糊指令下机器人意图定位不准、操作可靠性低。

Method: 提出 GIVE（Gesture Intent via Visual-Semantic Enhancement）框架，包含两条增强路径：1. 视觉路径：在机器人观测中叠加手部骨架和指尖射线，进行显式物体定位；2. 语义路径：生成人类手势与任务指令的高级语义描述，用于稳健的意图对齐。

Result: 在真实场景人机交互实验中，GIVE 相比基准模型，目标物体识别准确率提升了 40%，整体任务成功率提升了 80%，并表现出强大的鲁棒性和空间布局泛化能力。

Conclusion: GIVE 通过协同视觉（骨架/射线）与语义（手势描述）两种增强方式，在不改变 VLA 模型架构的前提下，显著提升了机器人对人类意图的理解能力，是实现更自然、可靠的人机协作（HRI）的有效方案。

Abstract: Human communication is inherently multimodal, where language is often accompanied by non-verbal cues such as gestures to convey intentions. However, current Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models treat robotic manipulation as a pure text-driven task, overlooking the important role of gestures in Human-Robot Interaction (HRI). This often leads to inaccurate intent grounding and unreliable manipulation when language instructions are ambiguous or underspecified. To address this challenge, we propose GIVE (Gesture Intent via Visual-Semantic Enhancement), an effective approach that enhances pre-trained VLA models with human gesture understanding without architectural modifications. Specifically, GIVE incorporates gesture information through two complementary pathways: a visual pathway that overlays hand skeletons and fingertip rays onto robot observations for explicit object grounding, and a semantic pathway that generates high-level descriptions of human gestures and task instructions for robust intent grounding. By jointly leveraging visual and semantic guidance, GIVE enables VLA policies to better associate gestures with manipulation behaviors and adapt to dynamic interaction intents. In real-world HRI experiments, GIVE substantially outperforms the baseline, improving target object recognition accuracy by 40% and overall task success rate by 80%, while demonstrating strong robustness and generalization to unseen spatial layouts and diverse participants.

</details>


### [119] [NavWAM: A Navigation World Action Model for Goal-Conditioned Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13494)
*Daichi Azuma,Taiki Miyanishi,Koya Sakamoto,Shuhei Kurita,Yaonan Zhu,Petr Khrapchenkov,Motoaki Kawanabe,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NavWAM 是一种基于扩散Transformer的导航策略，它通过将未来预测与动作决策联合建模，实现了无需外部规划的闭环视觉导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的导航世界模型通常仅作为预测模块，需要额外的外部规划器才能实现闭环控制，导致效率低下且缺乏集成度；研究旨在将视觉预测直接转化为可执行的机器人动作。

Method: 提出了一种基于扩散Transformer的策略（NavWAM），将未来观测、目标进度价值和动作块（action chunks）表示为统一的共享潜在序列；通过仿真预训练与实机适配，端到端学习从视觉预测到动作执行的映射。

Result: 在离线基准测试和真实机器人闭环部署中，NavWAM 的表现优于基于规划的世界模型基线，且无需复杂的搜索算法即可实现高效导航。

Conclusion: NavWAM 证明了通过联合学习未来预测、行为和目标价值，可以将视觉预测无缝转化为闭环控制，无需昂贵的在线规划（如 CEM），且能在真实机器人导航任务中实现优于传统规划式世界模型的性能。

Abstract: Goal-conditioned visual navigation requires a robot to act under partial observability by anticipating how its motion will change the future egocentric view and whether that change brings it closer to the goal. Navigation world models provide such visual foresight, but they remain prediction modules that require an external planner to convert predicted futures into closed-loop control. We propose Navigation World Action Model (NavWAM), a diffusion-transformer policy that turns navigation world-model prediction into executable action by representing future observations, goal-progress values, and action chunks in a shared latent sequence. By learning future prediction jointly with the action and value targets that determine closed-loop behavior, NavWAM makes visual foresight directly usable for robot control. We build NavWAM through simulation pretraining and real-robot adaptation, and evaluate it on image-goal navigation against planning-based world models and a representative direct navigation policy. Across offline benchmarks and closed-loop real-robot deployment, NavWAM improves over planning-based world-model baselines in our evaluations while using the default policy mode without CEM-style action search. Project page: https://dachii-azm.github.io/navwam/

</details>


### [120] [SPARC: Reliable Spatial Annotations from Robot Demonstrations at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13497)
*Nils Blank,Paul Mattes,Maximilian Xiling Li,Jakub Suliga,Thomas Roth,Moritz Reuss,Pankhuri Vanjani,Rudolf Lioutikov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了 SPARC 框架，通过引入时空结构一致性校准，提升了机器人演示自动标注的质量，在保证高精度的同时极大增加了可用样本量，从而提升了机器人具身模型在复杂场景下的感知与决策能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人演示自动化标注管线无法为生成的标签提供高质量的可靠性衡量标准，导致用户必须在“接受噪声数据”和“丢弃大量潜在有用样本”之间做权衡。

Method: 提出 SPARC 框架，通过引入可靠性校准机制，利用机器人任务的时空结构对自动生成的空间标注（边界框、轨迹、阶段标签）进行可靠性评分；并构建 IA-Bench 基准进行模型验证。

Result: 在 1.7k 条演示记录上，SPARC 的定位精度显著优于传统检测模型；在保持高精度要求下，数据保留率是基线的 3 倍；下游策略在真实世界复杂场景中表现优于传统方案。

Conclusion: SPARC 通过利用机器人任务的时空结构，有效解决了自动化轨迹标注中标签置信度低的问题，生成的标注数据可显著提升机器人具身模型和感知策略的性能，且无需人工介入。

Abstract: This work introduces Spatial Annotations from Robot Demonstrations with Reliability Calibration (SPARC), a risk-aware framework that automatically labels robot demonstrations with structured spatial annotations and assigns each annotation a reliability score. Structured spatial annotations, such as bounding boxes, object trajectories, and manipulation phase labels, benefit a broad range of robotics applications from training grounded robot policies and embodied foundation models to motion planning and hierarchical task composition. Existing automated pipelines generate such annotations at scale but provide no reliable quality signal: detector confidence is poorly calibrated for annotation correctness, forcing a choice between accepting noisy labels or discarding useful samples. In contrast to existing automated pipelines, SPARC leverages the spatio-temporal structure inherent to robot tasks to generate a reliability signal, reducing noisy labels and retaining more useful samples. We further introduce Interaction-Aware Bench (IA-Bench), a benchmark that measures model accuracy in grounding the locations of interacted objects in robot demonstrations. On 1.7k human-annotated demonstrations spanning diverse embodiments and scenarios, SPARC significantly outperforms detection-only baselines in localization accuracy while retaining three times more samples at high-precision operating points. Our experiments demonstrate that models finetuned on our annotations achieve state-of-the-art results on object-grounding and pointing benchmarks among similarly sized models, while remaining competitive on broader spatial-reasoning suites without manually verified or annotated training data. Furthermore, policies trained on SPARC-generated annotations outperform baselines in cluttered, visually ambiguous real-world scenes. Code, data, and models are available at intuitive-robots.github.io/sparc-labeling.

</details>


### [121] [MCR-Bionic Hand: Anatomical Structural Priors for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13601)
*Haosen Yang,Guowu Wei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于“结构智能”的MCR-Bionic Hand设计理念，通过精细复刻人类手部的肌骨解剖结构，实现了低维控制输入向高维灵巧动作的有效转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统灵巧手通常依赖高维主动控制，而人类手部的灵巧性部分源于其骨骼、韧带、肌腱及肌肉构成的物理架构（结构智能）。旨在探讨如何通过物理结构减轻控制系统的复杂性。

Method: 提出“结构智能”框架，包括“结构先验生成”（将低维输入转化为默认抓取配置）和“肌肉介导调节”（在默认状态下微调姿态和稳定性）；设计并研制了MCR-Bionic Hand（1:1肌骨仿生手），整合了骨骼结构、肌腱路径、韧带约束和内源性肌肉通路。

Result: MCR-Bionic Hand实现了腕部姿势诱导多关节预塑形、指伸肌腱帽耦合运动以及内源性肌肉路径对末端稳定性的调节；成功完成了硬币旋转、笔转移及立方体操纵等接触密集型任务。

Conclusion: 解剖学仿生学在机器人设计中的意义不仅在于视觉仿真，更在于通过结构实现部分控制功能（结构智能），从而降低控制维度。

Abstract: Dexterous robotic hands are usually formulated as high dimensional active control systems governed by degrees of freedom, actuation, and algorithms. Human hand dexterity, however, is partly encoded in the physical architecture of bones, ligaments, tendons, aponeuroses, and intrinsic muscles. This work describes that contribution as two linked forms of structural intelligence: structural prior generation, in which wrist to finger tenodesis, FDS/FDP routing, and the dorsal extensor hood transform low dimensional posture inputs into default grasp configurations and PIP to DIP coordination; and muscle mediated modulation, in which extrinsic muscles, lumbricals, and interossei regulate MCP posture, distal stability, fingertip force paths, and contact states around that default state.
  Based on this framework, MCR-Bionic Hand is developed as a 1:1 musculoskeletal biomimetic hand integrating a two row eight bone wrist, cross wrist tendons, anatomical flexor routing, volar plate and collateral ligament constraints, the dorsal extensor hood, and intrinsic muscle pathways within one body. Functional demonstrations and geometric mechanical models show that wrist posture induces multi joint pre shaping, the extensor hood maps PIP posture to a coupled DIP response, and intrinsic plus pathways modulate distal stability and fingertip action direction after grasp formation. Contact rich tasks, including coin rotation, pen transfer, dorsal coin flipping, and cube manipulation, show that MCR-Bionic links low dimensional state generation with fine post contact modulation. These results suggest that anatomical biomimetics is valuable not for visual similarity, but for identifying human hand structures that perform part of control.

</details>


### [122] [$\texttt{WEAVER}$, Better, Faster, Longer: An Effective World Model for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13672)
*Arnav Kumar Jain,Yilin Wu,Jesse Farebrother,Gokul Swamy,Andrea Bajcsy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 为了解决机器人世界模型在保真度、长程一致性和效率间的权衡难题，本文提出了 WEAVER。该模型利用多视角输入和流匹配技术，在机器人操纵任务中有效提升了策略评估、改进及实时规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界模型（WM）难以同时兼顾保真度、长程一致性和计算效率，导致其在机器人复杂动态操纵任务中的下游应用（策略评估、改进及规划）受限。

Method: 提出了一种多视角世界模型架构 WEAVER。其核心在于采用流匹配（flow-matching）损失函数来预测未来潜在表征和奖励值，并通过特定设计优化了模型架构、内存机制和预测目标，从而实现高效的长程轨迹模拟。

Result: WEAVER 在仿真和真实机器人平台上表现卓越：在策略评估中达到 0.870 的真实世界成功率相关性；在基础策略之上提升了 38% 的真实世界成功率；在测试时规划中实现了 14% 的成功率提升，且速度比现有方法快 5-10 倍；在分布外（OOD）场景中表现也优于现有模型。

Conclusion: WEAVER 成功在机器人操纵任务中同时满足了高保真度、长程一致性和计算效率的要求。该研究表明，集成多视角观测和流匹配目标是提升世界模型在复杂动态任务中表现的关键，并验证了其在硬件端的实际应用潜力。

Abstract: The potential impacts of world models (WMs, i.e., learned simulators) on robotics are far-reaching -- policy evaluation, policy improvement, and test-time planning -- all with limited real-world interaction. To unlock these downstream capabilities, a WM needs to jointly satisfy three desiderata: $\textit{(i)}$ fidelity (i.e., producing simulated trajectories that correlate with reality), $\textit{(ii)}$ consistency (i.e., producing simulated trajectories that are coherent over long horizons), and $\textit{(iii)}$ efficiency (i.e., producing simulated trajectories quickly). We propose $\texttt{WEAVER}$ (World Estimation Across Views for Embodied Reasoning): a WM architecture that simultaneously achieves all three desiderata, providing state-of-the-art results on robotic manipulation tasks. $\texttt{WEAVER}$ is a multi-view WM trained to predict future latents and reward values via a flow-matching loss. We distill the key design decisions across model architecture, memory, and prediction objectives required to unlock the kinds of long-horizon dynamic manipulation tasks that have confounded prior world modeling approaches. We apply $\texttt{WEAVER}$ in robotic hardware, demonstrating its effectiveness at policy evaluation ($ρ$=0.870 correlation with real-world success rate), policy improvement (real-world success rate improvement of $38\%$ on top of the $π_{0.5}$ robot foundation model), and test-time planning (real-world success rate improvement of $14\%$ with a $5-10\times$ speedup over prior WMs). $\texttt{WEAVER}$ also demonstrates better performance than prior WMs when evaluated on out-of-distribution scenarios. Code, models, and videos at: https://arnavkj1995.github.io/WEAVER/ .

</details>


### [123] [Improving Robotic Generalist Policies via Flow Reversal Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13675)
*Andy Tang,William Chen,Andrew Wagenmaker,Chelsea Finn,Sergey Levine*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出“流反转导向”（FRS），通过反向遍历流策略将次优指令映射至高效动作模式，从而增强机器人通用策略在零样本控制、任务蒸馏及强化学习中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的通用机器人策略虽具备多样化的行为先验，但在处理复杂新任务或直接控制失败时，缺乏一种有效的机制来推理、修正并调用这些先验能力以满足精确的指令需求。

Method: 提出“流反转导向”（Flow Reversal Steering, FRS）：该方法将次优动作通过流策略进行反向处理，推导并寻找相应的潜在噪声（latent noises），再通过正向映射将这些噪声转化为通用策略中的高效动作模式。同时利用此方法进行强化学习引导和行为克隆蒸馏。

Result: FRS 在模拟和真实机器人操作任务中表现优异：实现了零样本控制能力的显著提升；通过行为克隆蒸馏技术，在少于一分钟的训练内实现了高达 95% 的绝对任务成功率提升；在标准强化学习难以完成的任务中，通过语义知识引导成功实现了策略性能提升。

Conclusion: FRS 是一种有效的推理与优化机制，通过反向流动映射引导通用策略，显著提升了机器人在复杂任务中的零样本控制能力、数据蒸馏效率以及强化学习的表现，是将高级语义指导转化为底层具身控制的有效桥梁。

Abstract: Generalist policies can learn a wide range of skills from diverse robot datasets. In order to solve or improve on challenging news tasks, we need a way to infer and invoke the appropriate actions from the policy's rich behavioral prior, especially when directly commanding the policy fails. We focus on flow matching generalists and propose Flow Reversal Steering (FRS): a method that takes suboptimal but ``reasonable'' actions, finds their latent noises by passing them through the flow policy in reverse, and maps them to nearby generalist action modes. We evaluate FRS across many simulated and real-world manipulation settings. First, FRS can turn coarse semantic guidance from humans or vision-language models (VLMs) into corresponding good robot actions, improving zero-shot control. These gains can be distilled with behavioral cloning by training an auxiliary policy to output noises that the generalist maps to good actions -- showing up to 95% absolute task success rate boosts in under a minute of training. Finally, FRS enables policy improvement by bootstrapping reinforcement learning with semantic knowledge, improving on several tasks that standard RL fails to improve on.

</details>


### [124] [Mana: Dexterous Manipulation of Articulated Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13677)
*Zhao-Heng Yin,Guanya Shi,Pieter Abbeel,C. Karen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Mana是一个通过模拟动画流程解决灵巧机器人操纵articulated工具的框架，利用低成本的自动化数据生成与由粗到细的学习策略，实现了高效的Sim-to-Real迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对当前灵巧机器人技术在处理具有内部自由度和丰富接触交互的“铰接式工具（articulated tools）”时，面临的复杂物理建模难、学习策略困难以及实验成本高等瓶颈。

Method: 提出了Mana（Manipulation Animator）框架，采用由粗到细（coarse-to-fine）的流水线：首先利用程序化生成抓取关键帧，随后结合运动规划（motion planning）和强化学习（reinforcement learning）将关键帧转化为操作轨迹。该方法仅需极低的人工标注（<1分钟/工具）。

Result: Mana在四种不同规模和关节类型的articulated工具上成功实现了零样本（zero-shot）Sim-to-Real迁移，涵盖了抓取和手内操作，验证了该方案在处理复杂工具使用任务中的有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: Mana框架通过将复杂操纵问题转化为动画问题，实现了高效、可扩展的零样本Sim-to-Real迁移，为灵巧操作articulated工具提供了一种通用且低人工成本的解决方案。

Abstract: Articulated tool manipulation remains a major challenge in dexterous robotics due to the need to coordinate internal degrees of freedom and contact-rich interactions. While prior work has largely focused on rigid objects, articulated tool use remains underexplored because of its physical complexity and the difficulty of learning functional grasping and manipulation policies. We present Mana (Manipulation Animator), a general sim-to-real framework that reinterprets dexterous manipulation as an animation problem. Inspired by computer animation, Mana employs a coarse-to-fine pipeline that transforms procedurally-generated grasp keyframes into manipulation trajectories through motion planning and reinforcement learning. The data generation process is largely automatic, requiring only a few mouse clicks to specify functional affordances (<1 minute per tool). Across four articulated tools spanning different scales and joint types, Mana achieves zero-shot sim-to-real transfer for both grasping and in-hand manipulation, demonstrating a scalable approach to dexterous articulated tool use.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [Non-Parametric Dual-Manifold Mapping via 8-Bit Bounded Transformation Matrices: Challenging FP-centric Hardware Paradigms in Low-Energy AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13328)
*Lars Kopp*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的8位整数计算框架，通过双流形映射和位操作彻底消除浮点需求，在实现极高能效的同时具备极强的边缘抗毁性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度学习硬件（FP32/FP16/FP8）存在高昂的计算、热量与能耗成本；作者旨在寻求一种低能耗、无浮点、适合边缘计算的替代范式。

Method: 1. 双流形映射：利用空间流形（8192神经元）与Gabor池化结构流形（4096神经元）。
2. 整数化运算：采用Z矩阵变换，完全消除浮点乘法器，仅使用8位符号整数、位掩码和指针偏移。
3. 动态更新：通过带界限（[-127, 127]）更新机制及随机噪声注入实现学习。
4. 推理逻辑：基于方向符号电荷累积，配合固定的阈值处理（theta_reject=8.0, theta_cut=2.0）。

Result: 在90%的截断稀疏度和20%的随机节点破坏条件下，该模型仍能实现近乎完美的重建；成功在8位整数边界下实现了极其高效的类布尔执行逻辑。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种完全基于8位整数运算、无需训练的计算框架，通过双流形映射和位操作替代了高开销的浮点运算。该方案在大规模稀疏和节点损伤下仍保持了极高的鲁棒性，为神经形态边缘计算提供了一种摆脱对浮点GPU依赖的高效替代方案。

Abstract: Modern deep learning hardware paradigms rely heavily on computationally expensive floating-point arithmetic (FP32, FP16, and FP8), requiring massive thermal and energetic overheads to maintain gradient-based optimization. This paper introduces a non-parametric, training-free computational framework for dual-manifold mapping that operates strictly within an 8-bit signed integer boundary and leverages simple bitwise and accumulation logic. By mapping a Spatial Manifold (N_spatial = 8192 neurons) and a Gabor-pooled Structural Manifold (N_structural = 4096 neurons) through an integer-based transformation matrix (Z-matrix), we eliminate the need for floating-point multipliers. Inference is achieved via cache-friendly pointer offsets and bitwise masks, accumulating directional sign-charges using fixed thresholds (theta_reject = 8.0, theta_cut = 2.0). Learning is executed through a localized, bounded update mechanism restricted strictly within [-127, 127], modulated by stochastic noise injection. Both architectures demonstrate extreme holographic resilience, preserving near-perfect reconstruction via a global scaling factor under 90% truncation sparsity and 20% random node destruction. By reducing core AI inference to 8-bit boundaries and boolean-like execution, this framework outlines a paradigm shift toward neuromorphic edge-computing, directly questioning the long-term necessity of dense, floating-point-centric GPU accelerators.

</details>


### [126] [SupraSNN: Exploiting Synapse-Level Parallelism in Spiking Neural Network Accelerators through Co-Optimized Mapping and Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13354)
*Seyed Sadra Ghavami,Mohammad Hossein Nikkhah,Mohammad Rasoul Roshanshah,Saeed Safari*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: SupraSNN 借鉴计算机体系结构中的“超标量”设计，通过软硬件协同优化，实现了突触与神经元计算的物理脱钩及高度并行化，显著提升了 SNN 在 FPGA 上的运行效率与能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲神经网络（SNNs）虽具高能效潜力，但其大规模并行性难以在物理硬件上高效执行，受限于传统的串行处理范式。

Method: 提出了一种硬件-软件协同设计框架 SupraSNN：1. 硬件架构上采用超标量设计，通过多播树（Multi-Cast Tree）将脉冲路由至并行突触处理单元（SPU），并通过归并树（Merge Tree）集中处理神经元状态；2. 软件上采用分区和启发式调度框架，负责内存约束映射与突触执行顺序规划。

Result: 在MNIST任务上，实现了149 μs的推理延迟及0.025 mJ/图的能耗，相比现有FPGA加速器，延迟降低47.6%，能效提升5.6倍；在Spiking Heidelberg数据集上，实现1.41 ms的推理延迟。

Conclusion: SupraSNN 通过解耦突触和神经元计算，并采用超标量架构，成功解决了SNN在硬件部署中大规模并行性管理的难题，显著提升了能效和处理吞吐量，为SNN的高效硬件实现提供了范式。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) offer a brain-inspired path toward highly efficient computation, but their practical deployment is constrained by the challenge of managing and executing their massive parallelism on physical hardware. This problem mirrors the historical challenge in processor design of moving beyond serial execution, a barrier broken by superscalar architectures that dispatch multiple instructions to parallel functional units. Drawing inspiration from this paradigm, we introduce a hardware-software co-design framework that treats synaptic events as parallelizable micro-operations. We present SupraSNN, a superscalar-inspired architecture that achieves high synapse-level parallelism by physically decoupling synaptic and neuronal computations. Within this architecture, a Multi-Cast Tree routes spike data to multiple parallel Synapse Processing Units serve as the computational pipelines, while a Merge Tree consolidates distributed results for processing by a unified Neuron Unit--deliberately centralizing complex neuron state dynamics to mitigate hardware overhead and resource duplication. The efficacy of this architecture is enabled by a sophisticated partitioning and scheduling framework that first maps the SNN onto hardware respecting memory constraints, then heuristic scheduling determines the synaptic execution order, maximizing throughput and resource utilization. Implementing a feedforward SNN trained on MNIST (93.44% accuracy), SupraSNN achieves 149 $μs$ inference latency and 0.025 mJ per image (0.276 nJ per synapse) on the Xilinx Zynq XC7Z020 FPGA--delivering 47.6% lower latency and 5.6$\times$ better energy efficiency than prior FPGA-based SNN accelerators. Beyond vision tasks, a recurrent SNN on the Spiking Heidelberg Dataset (71.82% accuracy) achieves 1.41 ms latency and 0.77 mJ per sample on XC7Z030.

</details>


### [127] [ReSCom: A Reconfigurable Spiking Neural Network Accelerator Using Stochastic Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13560)
*Ali Alipour Fereidani,Mohammad Rasoul Roshanshah,Saeed Safari*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: ReSCom 是一种基于随机计算的脉冲神经网络（SNN）加速器，通过权衡精度、延迟和能耗，实现了高效且可重构的神经元计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲神经网络虽具能效潜力，但硬件实现面临计算功率与面积开销大的难题，且若缺乏有效的精度控制，直接进行近似计算会导致循环状态更新不稳定。

Method: 提出了一种可重构 SNN 加速器架构 ReSCom，在神经元动力学计算中利用随机计算处理乘法，保留定点运算处理加减法，并采用统一的可重构神经元设计（支持 IF、LIF 和突触模型）。

Result: 在 Xilinx Artix-7 FPGA 上进行 MNIST 推理实验，准确率达到 92.8%，单张图像操作能耗仅为 0.05 mJ（100 MHz 下），优于现有的先进实现方案。

Conclusion: 本文提出的 ReSCom 加速器通过混合随机计算和定点运算，在保证脉冲神经网络（SNN）推理稳定性的同时，显著提升了能效并实现了灵活的精度-延迟-能耗权衡。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) provide an attractive framework for energy-efficient inference due to their event-driven computation and biologically inspired dynamics. However, efficient hardware realization of SNNs remains challenging because neuronal computations incur significant power and area costs, and uncontrolled approximate arithmetic can destabilize recurrent state updates when precision is not properly managed. To address these challenges, this paper presents ReSCom, a reconfigurable SNN accelerator that leverages stochastic computing to reduce hardware complexity while maintaining stable inference. The proposed architecture employs stochastic arithmetic for multiplication operations in neuron dynamics, while preserving exact fixed-point addition/subtraction operations. This stochastic strategy enables runtime trade-offs between accuracy, latency, and energy consumption. A unified reconfigurable neuron design supports Integrate-and-Fire (IF), Leaky Integrate-and-Fire (LIF), and Synaptic neuron models within a single hardware framework. Experimental results for MNIST inference on a Xilinx Artix-7 FPGA show that ReSCom achieves $92.80\%$ classification accuracy while consuming just $0.05~\mathrm{mJ}$ of operational energy per image at $100~\mathrm{MHz}$, outperforming the energy efficiency of recent state-of-the-art implementations. Furthermore, managing the stochastic bit-stream length allows explicit, dynamic control over accuracy-latency-energy trade-offs to meet target application constraints.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [128] [ITME: Inference Tiered Memory Expansion with Disaggregated CXL-Hybrid Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12556)
*Hakbeom Jang,Younghoon Min,Sunwoong Kim,Taeyoung Ahn,Hanyee Kim,Youngpyo Joo,Hoshik Kim,Jongryool Kim*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对 LLM 长上下文带来的内存扩展瓶颈，本文提出了 ITME 系统，利用 CXL 远程内存技术实现 TB 级存算扩展，通过确定性数据管理显著优化了大规模上下文推理的吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 LLM 转向代理化（agentic）和长上下文任务，导致对 TB 级上下文状态的需求超出了单台服务器的容量；现有的存储方案（如 NVMe-oF 结合 DPU）存在软件优化复杂和成本高昂的问题。

Method: 提出了一种名为 ITME（Inference Tiered Memory Expansion）的系统，利用 CXL 混合内存技术实现 TB 级别的字节可寻址远程内存扩展；利用推理过程中模型权重和前缀缓存（prefix caches）的确定性访问模式，优化跨内存-存储层次结构的数据移动。

Result: 在基于 SK Hynix CMM 和 PCIe Gen5 NVMe SSD 的生产级设备及 FPGA 原型上进行了验证；测试表明 ITME 可提供超出宿主机内存限制的巨大 KV 缓存空间，并将系统吞吐量提升了高达 35.7%。

Conclusion: ITME 通过提供 TB 级别的字节可寻址远程内存扩展，有效解决了大型语言模型（LLM）分布式共享上下文基础设施的扩展难题，证明了在存储/内存层次结构中主动管理数据移动的有效性，为处理海量长上下文任务提供了一种高性能、高性价比的架构方案。

Abstract: The rapid shift toward agentic and long-context workloads in Large Language Models (LLMs) is pushing the industry beyond the capacity of individual servers toward disaggregated shared storage to handle TB-scale context states. This movement has led to the emergence of specialized shared context layers designed to externalize and share cumulative inference states across distributed clusters. While offloading to a data processing unit (DPU) within just-a-bunch-of-flash (JBOF) architectures accelerates NVMe-over-fabrics (NVMe-oF) target processing, the need for sophisticated software-level optimization and cost-efficiency burdens remain significant. Consequently, the ideal architecture for scaling this shared context infrastructure is still an active area of exploration. In this paper, we propose ITME (Inference Tiered Memory Expansion), which leverages a CXL-hybrid memory to present a massive, TB-scale byte-addressable remote memory expansion. This approach enables cost-efficient scaling and simplifies the software stack through direct byte-addressability, effectively addressing the challenges of shared context infrastructure. Our key insight is that the deterministic access patterns of voluminous model weights and prefix caches enable the system to proactively manage data movement across the memory-storage hierarchy. We validate ITME by evaluating its performance potential with production-grade SK Hynix CMM and PCIe Gen5 NVMe SSDs, while further demonstrating its functional feasibility through an FPGA-based hardware prototype. Overall, ITME enhances conventional CPU-offloading by providing additional remote memory expansion to accommodate large KV cache footprints beyond host memory limits, achieving up to a 35.7\% throughput improvement.

</details>


### [129] [Eidola: Modeling Multi-GPU Network Communication Traffic in Distributed AI Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12638)
*Ranganath R. Selagamsetty,Matthew Poremba,Bradford M. Beckmann,Joshua San Miguel,Mikko H. Lipasti*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Eidola 是一个基于 gem5 的可扩展扩展，通过利用定时配置文件模拟周期级的多 GPU 通信流量，解决了大规模分布式 AI 系统中由于复杂同步和通信机制带来的建模难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着分布式 AI 模型规模的扩大，内核融合（kernel fusion）和计算通信重叠等技术引入了难以建模的不规则瞬态流量。现有仿真工具难以准确评估这些技术在细粒度同步和 P2P 通信上对互连带宽和延迟带来的压力。

Method: 开发了一个名为 Eidola 的 gem5 扩展工具。它利用真实应用中的标注定时配置文件（annotated timing profiles）进行周期级（cycle-level）的 P2P GPU 写操作仿真，通过“eidolon”（模型简化）方式模拟大规模多 GPU 配置下的通信流量。

Result: 成功复现了融合内核执行中的可变性，并实现了受 SyncMon 启发的同步机制，验证了其在减少轮询相关内存流量方面的作用。实验表明该工具有效且可扩展。

Conclusion: Eidola 为研究分布式多 GPU 系统中的细粒度通信提供了一个灵活且可扩展的仿真平台，有效弥补了现有工具在处理复杂、不规则流量模式建模方面的不足。

Abstract: As distributed AI workloads grow in scale, multi-GPU systems have become essential for training large models. Although techniques like kernel fusion and overlapping communication with computation help reduce delays, they also introduce irregular and transient traffic patterns that are difficult to model using existing tools. These techniques rely heavily on fine-grained synchronization and peer-to-peer communication, which place significant pressure on interconnect bandwidth and latency.
  In this work, we introduce Eidola, a scalable extension to the gem5 simulation framework that enables detailed modeling of inter-GPU communication traffic. The extension is scalable as our GPU model serves as a succinct eidolon, emulating the minimal characteristics needed for traffic modeling. Eidola uses annotated timing profiles from real applications to emulate peer-to-peer GPU writes with cycle-level precision. This allows researchers to simulate and analyze synchronization behavior across large multi-GPU configurations. The simulator supports configurable per-GPU traffic patterns and enables isolated performance analysis under different communication scenarios.
  We demonstrate Eidola's effectiveness by reproducing variability in fused kernel execution and by implementing a SyncMon-inspired synchronization mechanism, confirming reductions in polling-related memory traffic. Our results show that Eidola provides a flexible and scalable platform for studying inter-GPU communication and supports architectural exploration in modern distributed GPU systems.

</details>


### [130] [GF-DiT: Scheduling Parallelism for Diffusion Transformer Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13501)
*Xinwei Qiang,Yifan Hu,Shixuan Sun,Jing Yang,Han Zhao,Chen Chen,Yu Feng,Jingwen Leng,Minyi Guo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 为解决扩散模型（DiT）推理中静态并行配置带来的低效问题，该论文提出了GF-DiT运行时系统，通过异步任务拆解与轻量级通信重构，实现了请求粒度的动态弹性并行，显著降低了延迟并提升了系统吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散模型（DiT）服务系统为请求分配固定的并行配置，无法适应DiT任务在请求内容、执行阶段及系统负载上的高度异构性，导致GPU利用率低与服务质量（QoS/SLO）下降。

Method: 1. 提出异步执行抽象：将扩散模型请求拆解为可独立调度的“轨迹任务”（trajectory tasks），实现实时GPU资源重新分配。2. 引入Group-Free Collectives：一种轻量级通信抽象，支持低开销（μs级）的在线通信组重构与配置。

Result: 在vLLM-Omni中集成并进行测试：吞吐量提升最高达6.01倍，平均延迟降低最高95%，SLO违规率降低最高90%，通信组建立开销从778ms大幅降低至约60μs。

Conclusion: GF-DiT通过将GPU并行度转变为动态可调的资源，解决了现有DiT服务系统中静态并行配置导致的低效问题，证明了弹性运行时在提升大模型推理性能方面的关键价值。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have become the dominant architecture for image and video generation, creating growing demand for efficient DiT serving. Existing systems assign each request a fixed parallel configuration throughout its lifetime. However, DiT workloads exhibit substantial heterogeneity across requests, execution stages, and system conditions, making static parallelism inefficient and often leading to poor GPU utilization and degraded service quality.
  This paper argues that DiT serving should treat GPU parallelism as a first-class schedulable resource. We present GF-DiT, a policy-programmable runtime for elastic DiT serving that dynamically adapts the parallelism of running requests according to workload demands and service objectives. GF-DiT introduces an asynchronous execution abstraction that decomposes requests into independently schedulable trajectory tasks and enables online GPU reallocation. To make elastic parallelism practical, GF-DiT further proposes group-free collectives, a lightweight communication abstraction that supports low-overhead online formation and reconfiguration of arbitrary execution groups.
  We implement GF-DiT in vLLM-Omni and evaluate it on representative image and video diffusion workloads. Compared with fixed-pipeline execution with static parallelism, GF-DiT improves throughput by up to 6.01$\times$, reduces mean latency by up to 95%, lowers SLO violation rates by up to 90%, and reduces communication-group setup overhead from 778 ms to approximately 60 $μ$s.

</details>


### [131] [On the Limits of Performance Portability in Directive-Based GPU Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12753)
*Alessandro Romeo,Nitin Shukla,Stefano Truzzi,Alessio Suriano,Andrea Mignone*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本研究通过 gPLUTO 代码移植实验发现，尽管基于指令的编程模型（OpenMP）具备跨平台能力，但在不同架构（NVIDIA 与 AMD）间存在严重性能鸿沟，受限于内存延迟、编译器后端能力及 C++ 抽象带来的寄存器压力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学应用在向 GPU 加速的 exascale 系统迁移时，在性能、可移植性和开发生产力之间存在难以平衡的矛盾。本研究旨在剖析基于指令的编程模型（Directive-based programming）在不同 GPU 架构下的性能可移植性现状。

Method: 将生产级的磁流体动力学（MHD）代码 gPLUTO 从 OpenACC 移植到 OpenMP；在 NVIDIA A100 (Leonardo Booster) 和 AMD MI250X (LUMI-G) 平台上进行对比测试；利用内核级 profiling 分析内存访问模式、寄存器压力以及编译器效率。

Result: NVIDIA 平台上 OpenACC 与 OpenMP 性能相当；在 AMD MI250X 上，OpenMP 代码表现不佳，应用级性能约为 NVIDIA 的 1/3，内核级存在高达一个数量级的性能差距，主要受限于步长内存访问模式（strided access）和编译器限制；由于 C++ 抽象层导致的寄存器压力和溢出，部分低并行度内核出现高达 47 倍的性能衰减。

Conclusion: 在GPU加速的百亿亿次（exascale）系统中实现性能可移植性（performance portability）仍然面临巨大挑战。指令式编程模型（OpenMP/OpenACC）在不同硬件架构（NVIDIA vs AMD）间存在显著性能差异，编译器优化和架构感知分析对于实现跨平台的性能优化至关重要。

Abstract: The transition of scientific applications to GPU-accelerated exascale systems is constrained by trade-offs between performance, portability, and productivity. This work evaluates the performance portability of directive-based GPU programming by porting gPLUTO, a production-grade magnetohydrodynamics code for astrophysical simulations, from OpenACC to OpenMP, and analyzing its performance on NVIDIA A100 (Leonardo Booster) and AMD MI250X (LUMI-G) devices. On NVIDIA platforms, OpenACC and OpenMP achieve comparable performance due to a shared compiler backend, providing a consistent baseline for assessing algorithmic efficiency. In contrast, the same OpenMP implementation is approximately three times slower at the application level on AMD MI250X with respect to the NVIDIA A100 OpenACC baseline, with kernel-level slowdowns reaching up to an order of magnitude, driven by sensitivity to strided memory-access patterns and compiler limitations. Kernel-level profiling shows that the dominant contributors to run-time are memory-latency-bound rather than limited by peak band-width. In low-parallelism kernels, C++ abstraction layers increase register pressure and spilling, leading to extreme slowdowns of up to 47x in specific cases. These results indicate that portable performance across GPU architectures requires not only application-level changes but also continued advances in compiler backends and architecture-aware optimization strategies

</details>


### [132] [SemanticXR: Low Power and Real-time Queryable Semantic Mapping with an Object-Level Device-Cloud Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12849)
*Rahul Singh,Devdeep Ray,Connor Smith,Sarita Adve*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对移动XR设备资源受限的痛点，SemanticXR通过将语义对象抽象为设备-云端协同的核心单位，实现了轻量级、实时且适应网络抖动的开放词汇语义映射与查询系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语义映射方案计算密集且依赖强算力设备，难以满足移动XR设备对“开放词汇、实时性、低功耗”的部署需求。目前的云卸载方案缺乏对资源受限环境下设备-云端处理流程（映射、存储、通讯）的有效管理。

Method: 提出一种基于“对象级（Object-level）”协同的设备-云端系统架构：在服务器端实施对象级并行化、几何下采样及深度映射协同设计以降低延迟与带宽；在设备端引入基于对象级的稀疏局部地图、增量更新及优先级管理机制，以优化带宽占用、查询延迟和内存开销，并支持动态资源调度。

Result: 相比基准方案，SemanticXR将服务器端映射延迟优化了2.2倍，上行带宽维持在2.5 Mbps以内；设备端查询延迟低于100ms（支持1万个对象），内存占用控制在500 MB以内，且仅增加约2%的设备功耗。

Conclusion: SemanticXR验证了通过将语义对象作为设备-云端协同工作的核心单元，能够在严苛的资源约束下实现实时、开放词汇的语义映射与查询，为移动XR应用落地提供了可行的系统架构。

Abstract: Semantic mapping is a core service that enables grounded interactions in emerging Extended Reality (XR) applications such as AI assistants and spatial object search. Deploying this capability on mobile XR devices requires a system that is open-vocabulary, real-time, and low-power. Existing approaches are compute-intensive and assume server-class resources. Cloud offloading offers a practical path, but no existing system splits semantic mapping across the device-cloud boundary or manages its communication, execution, and memory footprint.
  We present SemanticXR, the first device-cloud system for real-time, open-vocabulary semantic mapping and querying under XR power, bandwidth, and memory constraints. Our key insight is to elevate semantically identifiable objects to first-class units of communication, execution, and memory across the device and server. On the server, object-level parallelism and geometry downsampling improve mapping latency, while object-level depth-mapping co-design reduces upstream bandwidth. On the device, an object-level sparse local map with incremental updates and update prioritization enables network-robust querying with bounded memory and downstream bandwidth. Object-level configurable resource usage vs. quality trade-offs let applications and the system adapt mapping to application requirements and operating conditions, respectively.
  Against a device-cloud baseline with the same perception models, object-level organization improves server-side mapping latency by 2.2X at equal semantic quality. Depth-mapping co-design maintains upstream bandwidth under 2.5 Mbps. On the device, SemanticXR sustains sub-100 ms query latency for up to 10,000 objects even under network drops, supports tens of thousands of objects within 500 MB, and scales downstream bandwidth with map changes, not total scene size. The system adds only 2% device power during normal operation.

</details>


### [133] [High-Order Spectral Element Methods for Wave Propagation on ARM Multicore CPU with SME: Optimizations and Implications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12850)
*Yinuo Wang,Lin Gan,Tianqi Mao,Wubing Wan,Zekun Yin,Wenqiang Wang,Wei Xue,Guangwen Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对 ARM 平台的 SME 架构优化了 SPECFEM3D，通过专用的矩阵内核、内存优化及 $(h,p)$ 策略调整，实现了 4-6 倍的性能提升并优化了数值离散的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的谱元法（SEM）实现无法充分发挥新兴 ARM 多核 CPU 中可扩展矩阵扩展（SME）架构的性能优势。

Method: 1. 针对 SME 开发了专门的批量小矩阵运算内核；2. 针对有限 HBM（高带宽内存）系统设计了内存感知的混合 MPI+OpenMP 执行方案；3. 进行了基于色散误差（dispersion-based）的 $(h,p)$ 等精度对比研究。

Result: 在固定多项式阶数下，该优化方案相比原始代码全应用性能提升 4-6 倍，并显著优于优化后的非 SME CPU 基准，成功实现性能量级的跨越。

Conclusion: SME 不仅提升了内核计算效率，还通过调整谱元法（SEM）的 $h$ 和 $p$ 离散化权衡，通过采用更高的多项式阶数，进一步降低了求解时间和工作集大小，从而改变了 ARM 平台上的数值模拟策略。

Abstract: Wave propagation based on the spectral element method (SEM) is a representative HPC workload, but existing SEM implementations are not well matched to emerging ARM multicore CPUs with Scalable Matrix Extension (SME). We present an SME-enabled optimization of \textsc{SPECFEM3D} on the emerging LX2 processor that combines an SME-aware batched small-matrix kernel for SEM tensor-product operators, a memory-aware hybrid MPI+OpenMP execution scheme for limited-HBM systems, and a dispersion-based iso-accuracy study of the $(h,p)$ tradeoff. At fixed polynomial order, the optimized implementation improves full-application performance by 4--6$\times$ over the original code and delivers clear gains over optimized non-SME CPU baselines. Beyond these implementation-level gains, our results suggest that SME shifts the performance-favorable operating point toward higher polynomial orders along the dispersion-based iso-accuracy frontier, further reducing time-to-solution and working-set size. These results indicate that SME affects not only kernel efficiency, but also the practical discretization tradeoff for SEM on modern ARM multicore platforms.

</details>


### [134] [Maestro: Workload-Aware Cross-Cluster Scheduling for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12950)
*Jinghao Wang,Xiao Zhou,Xiaoyang Sun,Yihui Zhang,Yilong Li,Tianyu Wo,Xu Wang,Chunming Hu,Renyu Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Maestro 是一个针对大规模 LLM 多智能体系统设计的资源调度器，通过利用智能体语义信息进行预测与分层调度，解决了算力瓶颈与内存碎片化问题，实现了资源利用率与服务性能的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 多智能体系统（LLM-MAS）在处理复杂任务时会导致资源消耗激增，且面临解码阶段成本不确定、多模型内存碎片化过量部署以及跨集群调度权衡等挑战，现有的资源调度策略难以胜任。

Method: 提出了一种基于工作负载意识（workload-aware）的分层调度系统：在单节点层面，利用权重缓存与弹性内存分配实现多模型共存；在集群层面，进行感知延迟的路由优化；在全局层面，执行基于工作流优先级的调度以减少排队阻塞。其核心在于通过语义分析预测各阶段的输出长度与内存需求，指导调度决策。

Result: 实验结果表明，Maestro 将 KV 缓存占用的 HBM（高带宽内存）降低了 67.2%，在高竞争环境下，相比 EDF（最早截止时间优先）调度策略，目标服务等级协议（SLO）达标率提升了 23.6 个百分点。

Conclusion: Maestro 通过针对 LLM 多智能体架构的语义感知调度与资源管理，显著降低了 HBM 开销并提升了高负载下的 SLO 达标率，为云端大规模 LLM-MAS 服务提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Model based Multi-Agent Systems (LLM-MAS) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for tackling complex tasks by breaking them into collaborative workflows of specialized LLM-powered agents. However, deploying such multi-agent workloads at scale poses significant system challenges. Each user query spawns an iterative pipeline of LLM calls, greatly amplifying resource consumption compared to single-turn queries. In resource-constrained cloud settings, these workflows face non-deterministic and input-dependent costs at decode stage, heavy-tailed multi-model requirements with memory fragmentation and over-provisioning, and cross-cluster scheduling trade-offs. We present Maestro, a workload-aware scheduling system designed for LLM-MAS serving under strict GPU budgets. Maestro explicitly leverages agent semantics and roles: it predicts the output length and memory usage of each stage and uses this prediction to drive a hierarchical scheduler. At the node level, Maestro enables dynamic multi-model co-location via hierarchical weight caching and elastic memory provisioning. At the cluster level, it performs latency-aware routing to avoid cold-start delays and memory overloads. At the global level, it enforces workflow-aware prioritization to minimize head-of-line blocking for interactive tasks. Across prototype experiments and trace-driven simulations, Maestro reduces KV-reservation HBM by 67.2% and improves high-contention SLO attainment over EDF by 23.6 percentage points.

</details>


### [135] [JiRAIYA: A Reputation-Based Hierarchical Federated Learning Framework on Web3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13180)
*Venkata Raghava Kurada,Pallav Kumar Baruah*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Web3技术的联邦学习框架，通过分层共识与声誉评价机制，解决了FL系统中聚合过程不透明、不可审计及安全性不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前企业级联邦学习（FL）系统存在聚合过程不透明、审计受限等问题，且现有的缓解策略往往带来额外的计算和通信开销。需要一种新技术（Web3）来提升训练过程的透明度、可信度与审计能力。

Method: 1. 引入分层架构（由代理管理器协调各联合体）；2. 采用基于共识机制的验证流程（独立评估模型更新）；3. 利用异常检测技术识别恶意更新；4. 引入基于声誉分数的备份机制以确保模型生成。

Result: 实验表明，该框架在真实场景下具有良好的有效性和鲁棒性，能够有效抵御对抗性攻击和数据投毒攻击，且在透明度方面优于传统FL系统。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于Web3的联邦学习框架通过去中心化的共识机制与声誉系统，有效地解决了传统FL系统中透明度不足与审计困难的问题，并能在对抗性环境下保持模型的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Federated Learning(FL) is predominantly deployed in enterprise environments, where limited transparency and restricted auditability hinder broader adoption. Existing FL systems often suffer from opaque aggregation processes, making it unclear which model updates are accepted or discarded. Current mitigation strategies typically rely on external validators introducing additional computational and communication overhead. In this paper, we propose a novel FL framework that leverages existing Web3 technologies to enhance transparency, trust and auditability throughout the training process. The framework adopts a hierarchical architecture in which delegated managers orchestrate the FL training process within their respective federations. To mitigate adversarial and poisoning attacks, a combination of novelty detection and consensus mechanisms were employed. Model updates are encoded and broad casted to all managers, who independently evaluate their validity and those model updates that are approved by the consensus are incorporated into the global model. Additionally, a reputation score based backup mechanism is employed to ensure model generation. Extensive experiments conducted under real world scenarios demonstrate the effectiveness, resilience of the proposed framework, highlighting its potential to enable transparent FL beyond traditional enterprise setting.

</details>


### [136] [Dynamic Resource Management in Production HPC Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13266)
*Petter Sandås,Sergio Iserte,Guillaume Houzeaux,Antonio J. Peña*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种实用的MPI可伸缩性框架（DMR），通过非侵入式集成方式，使大规模科学应用能在生产级HPC系统上动态调整资源，从而在保持性能的同时大幅降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前大规模科学应用呈现出显著的时间动态性，但现有的HPC集群主要依赖固定的资源分配模式，而现有的动态调整技术多局限于实验室阶段，缺乏在生产环境中的实用性和易用性。

Method: 开发了动态资源管理（DMR）框架，通过非侵入式方式集成至MPI应用程序，使其能根据应用行为调整资源分配，且无需修改现有的调度器，并已在三台TOP500超级计算机上进行了实际部署验证。

Result: 通过DMR框架在两项大型科学应用中测试，实验表明在生产环境中实现了与静态分配相当的性能，并显著降低了相同工作负载下的节点小时（node-hour）能耗。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了在生产级HPC环境中实现MPI可伸缩性（malleability）是切实可行的，且无需对现有调度器进行侵入式修改。通过DMR框架，该方案有效地降低了资源消耗，为HPC领域广泛采用动态资源管理提供了路径。

Abstract: Many large-scale scientific applications exhibit time-varying behavior, yet production HPC clusters still rely on rigid, fixed-size allocations, and most dynamic techniques remain confined to laboratory prototypes. This work presents a practical MPI malleability methodology that integrates with state-of-the-art high-performance computing (HPC) software stacks and operational practices. The methodology is implemented in the Dynamic Management of Resources (DMR) framework and is designed to ease adoption by existing applications without requiring intrusive code changes or scheduler modifications. We evaluate our approach by integrating the DMR API into two large-scale scientific applications and deploying them on three TOP500 supercomputers under realistic production configurations. Our non-invasive malleability solution achieves performance comparable to static baselines in controlled environments while substantially reducing node-hour consumption for identical workloads. These results show that malleability can be effectively exploited on production systems using vanilla resource managers, lowering the barrier to adoption of dynamic resource management in HPC.

</details>


### [137] [Skiplists with Foresight: Skipping Cache Misses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13321)
*Tomer Cory,Niv Sulimany,Erez Petrank*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Foresight 是一种提升跳表缓存友好性的优化方案，通过解决并发同步挑战，在各种跳表设计中实现了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跳表作为数据存储索引的核心结构，其性能受限于缓存命中率。研究旨在通过缓存优化提升跳表在不同架构下的执行效率，特别是解决在并发场景下引入优化所带来的同步挑战。

Method: 提出了一种名为 Foresight 的缓存优化技术，并针对并发环境下的同步问题提出了相应的解决方案，将其集成至多种序列及并发跳表设计（如 DBx1000 中的索引）中进行验证。

Result: 在微基准测试中，吞吐量最高提升 45%；在 DBx1000 内存数据库中，端到端性能最高提升 15%。

Conclusion: Foresight 是一种轻量级且易于集成的跳表优化方案，通过提升缓存友好性，在单线程和多线程场景下均能显著提升跳表的吞吐性能。

Abstract: A skiplist is a fundamental data structure widely used in systems and applications for indexing data stores. In this work, we introduce Foresight, a cache-friendly skiplist optimization. Extending Foresight to concurrent settings introduces significant synchronization challenges that we identify and address. Foresight is a surgical optimization, easy to integrate into a wide variety of skiplist designs. We apply it to one sequential and three concurrent skiplist designs and observe throughput improvements of up to 45% in microbenchmarks. When applied to a skiplist-based index in the DBx1000 in-memory database, Foresight yields end-to-end performance gains of up to 15%.

</details>


### [138] [Work Stealing for the 2D-Mesh Topology of Satellite Constellations in Low Earth Orbit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13329)
*Mia Reitz,Dorian Chenet,Jonas Posner*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对空间边缘计算（SEC）的“仅限邻居”任务窃取策略，有效规避了卫星间多跳通信的高延迟，并在保持良好负载均衡的前提下，在大规模部署中表现出优于全局窃取的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决异步多任务（AMT）模型直接应用于具有稀疏网格拓扑和高时延特性的空间边缘计算（SEC/LEO卫星星座）时，全局工作窃取产生的过高通信代价问题。

Method: 提出了一种“仅限邻居”（neighbor-only）的工作窃取策略，改变了传统AMT（异步多任务）模型中随机全局选择受害者的机制；通过分析模型评估时延优势，并在HPC集群上利用模拟的网格拓扑进行实验验证。

Result: 分析模型显示，随星座规模增加，受限窃取带来的单次尝试时延优势愈发明显；实测结果表明，在均衡及不均衡负载下，邻居策略的性能与全局策略仅相差约2.2%。

Conclusion: 在空间边缘计算（SEC）场景下，邻居限定的任务窃取策略能够有效降低通信延迟，且在负载均衡性能上与全局窃取策略相当，是支持大规模卫星星座计算的可行方案。

Abstract: Asynchronous Many-Task (AMT) is a parallel programming model used in High Performance Computing (HPC). An AMT runtime can distribute fine-grained tasks across processing units called workers, through work stealing: when a worker has no tasks left to process, it tries to steal tasks from other workers. Workers are not restricted to a single compute node but can also be distributed across multiple nodes of an HPC cluster. Existing AMT runtimes assume a fully connected network with low, uniform latency and perform global work stealing, selecting another worker at random from all workers in the system.
  Space Edge Computing (SEC) uses constellations of satellites in Low Earth Orbit (LEO) as distributed compute clusters. Unlike HPC clusters, LEO satellites communicate through inter-satellite links that form a sparse mesh topology. Reaching a distant satellite requires multiple hops, each adding latency.
  As a step toward adapting AMT to SEC, this paper proposes a neighbor-only work stealing strategy in which workers steal exclusively from directly connected neighbors, avoiding multi-hop communication. An analytical model shows that restricting stealing this way yields a per-attempt latency advantage that grows with constellation size. Preliminary experiments on an HPC cluster with an emulated mesh over uniform low-latency links isolate the effect of victim selection: the neighbor-only strategy performs within ~2.2% of global stealing on both balanced and irregular workloads, indicating that restricting the victim set does not harm load balancing in this setting. Taken together, the experiments suggest that neighbor-only stealing can be on a par with global stealing, and the model suggests that neighbor-only stealing becomes preferable at scale.

</details>


### [139] [Temporal Conductance and Bounds on the Voter Model for Dynamic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13374)
*Tatiana Rocha Avila,Holger Dell,John Lapinskas*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的动态网络连通性度量指标“时间导度（temporal conductance）”，并将投票者模型达到共识的预期时间边界从静态网络成功推广至动态网络，证明了该边界的紧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的投票者模型分析主要依赖于静态导度，但静态导度在动态网络（尤其是某些快照断开的网络）中失效；需要一种更具鲁棒性的指标来刻画动态网络的连通性，从而分析其在该结构下的共识时间。

Method: 引入 temporal conductance $Φ$ 来替换静态网络中的导度，该度量能够捕获在不同时间片段出现的连接；通过理论推导证明了投票者模型在动态网络下达到共识的预期时间上限为 $O(m/(d_{\min}Φ))$。

Result: 证明了投票者模型在动态网络中达到共识的预期时间上限为 $O(m/(d_{\min}Φ))$，并且证明了该理论结果是紧的（tight），即达到了最优上限。

Conclusion: 通过引入“时间导度（temporal conductance）”这一新指标，研究者成功地将静态网络中投票者模型的收敛时间边界推广到了更具一般性的动态网络，且证明了该边界在常数因子内是紧的。该指标有望成为分析时变网络及时间非均匀马尔可夫链的重要工具。

Abstract: The voter model is a classical stochastic process that models how opinions might spread through a network: at each step, every node lazily adopts the opinion of a random neighbour; eventually all nodes share the same opinion (consensus). Stronger connectivity should yield faster consensus. Berenbrink, Giakkoupis, Kermarrec, and Mallmann-Trenn (ICALP 2016) make this precise via the network's conductance: if the network has $m$ edges, minimum degree $d_{\min}$, and conductance at least $φ$, then the voter model reaches consensus in expected $O(m/(d_{\min}φ))$ steps. Their results extend to dynamic networks with fixed vertex degrees by considering the network's conductance at each time step.
  We introduce temporal conductance $Φ$, a more general connectivity measure for dynamic networks. Unlike static conductance, which collapses to $0$ whenever some snapshot is disconnected, $Φ$ captures connectivity through edges that appear at different times. We generalise the results of Berenbrink et al. from static conductance to temporal conductance, showing that the expected consensus time of the standard voter model is at most $O(m/(d_{\min}Φ))$. Moreover, we prove that this bound is tight up to constant factors. We expect temporal conductance to be a useful primitive for analysing other dynamics on temporal networks, and potentially time-inhomogeneous Markov chains more generally.

</details>


### [140] [Finding Conservation Laws of Large Dynamical Systems with Tasks and Futures: A Case Study in Utilizing Dynamic Data Dependencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13623)
*Rüdiger Nather*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对 Future 模型因值不可变限制影响高性能线性代数计算内存复用的问题，本文提出了 await_delete 机制，在 Taskflow 中实现了高效的矩阵分块求逆，验证了其在大规模计算中的线性扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行工作负载日益复杂，传统的 Future 模型虽擅长处理不规则算法的依赖关系，但其值不可变（immutability）的限制导致难以执行高性能线性代数所需的原地（in-place）内存更新和回收。

Method: 1. 提出新抽象 await_delete：在传统 Future 语义上扩展，允许在消费者完成后安全地重用内存。
2. 算法设计：基于该机制设计了针对稠密对称矩阵的分块求逆算法。
3. 实现与评估：在 Taskflow 的扩展版本中实现，并进行强扩展性（strong-scaling）实验。

Result: 1. 验证了 await_delete 有效支持了矩阵求逆中的内存复用。
2. 实验表明，虽然在小规模任务上 Future 存在较高开销，但在处理大规模矩阵时，该方法实现了近乎线性的扩展性。
3. 给出了 Future 在高性能计算中应用的摊销阈值分析。

Conclusion: 通过引入 await_delete 语义，使得基于 Future 的并行编程模型能够在保持任务依赖管理优势的同时，通过安全的值重用和内存回收机制，高效支持高性能线性代数计算。对于大规模任务，该模型能实现近乎线性的扩展性。

Abstract: As parallel workloads grow in complexity, managing fine-grained data dependencies becomes a critical challenge. Futures offer a promising model for handling these dependencies, particularly in irregular algorithms, but they also come with the restriction of value-immutability. This immutability limits the ability to perform in-place memory updates, a necessity for high-performance linear algebra where memory recycling is paramount.
  In this paper, we address these limitations by introducing a new construct, await_delete, which extends traditional future semantics to allow safe value reuse once consumers are finished. Building on this extension, we present a novel future-based algorithm for the block-wise inversion of dense, symmetric matrices, motivated by a recent algorithm for finding conservation laws of dynamical systems.
  We implement our approach in an extended version of Taskflow and evaluate it through strong-scaling experiments. Our results demonstrate that while futures incur significant overhead on smaller problem sizes, they achieve nearly linear scaling on large matrices. We analyze the amortization threshold and show that futures are a viable high-performance tool for large-scale linear algebra.

</details>


<div id='cs.PF'></div>

# cs.PF [[Back]](#toc)

### [141] [Beyond Virtual Delay: Improving Packet Delay Bound in Network Calculus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13631)
*Yuming Jiang*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 本文指出了网络演算中经典时延界限的保守性，并提出了一种无需额外假设的改进型包时延界限，在理论和实际案例中均优于经典方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典网络演算中的时延界限基于虚拟时延（Virtual Delay）概念，该方法在分析实际数据包时延时存在固有的过度估计（保守性），需要更精确的界限分析方法。

Method: 通过理论推导证明数据包最大时延始终小于或等于虚拟最大时延；利用到达曲线（Arrival Curve）和业务曲线（Service Curve）直接推导出了新的数据包时延上限，无需额外假设。

Result: 在漏桶（Leaky-bucket）到达曲线和速率-时延（Rate-latency）服务曲线构成的系统中，新界限在数学上严格优于经典界限，并已通过时间敏感网络（TSN）案例研究验证。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了经典基于虚拟时延的界限在分析数据包时延时存在固有的保守性，并推导出了一个新的、更精确的数据包时延界限，在多种场景（如TSN）下表现优于经典界限。

Abstract: In network calculus, a fundamental result is the classical delay bound given by the horizontal deviation between the arrival and service curves. While widely used, the classical bound is derived from the notion of virtual delay. In this work, we first show that the maximum packet delay is always upper-bounded by the maximum virtual delay, revealing inherent conservatism when applying the virtual-delay-based bound to packet delay. Motivated by this insight, we revisit packet delay analysis and derive a new packet delay bound that requires no assumptions beyond the arrival and service curves. Specializing the new bound to a system with leaky-bucket arrival curve and rate-latency service curve shows strict improvement over the classical bound, which is further demonstrated through a case study in time-sensitive networking (TSN).

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [142] [TrajGenAgent: A Hierarchical LLM Agent for Human Mobility Trajectory Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12657)
*Siyu Li,Toan Tran,Lingyi Zhao,Khurram Shafique,Li Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TrajGenAgent 是一个无需微调的层次化LLM智能体框架，通过将“语义活动规划”与“确定性运动学落地”相结合，高效地生成了兼具统计精度与行为合理性的真实人类轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的轨迹生成方法存在局限性：提示工程虽然零样本但缺乏细粒度的时空接地；微调方法虽精准但计算成本高且可能损失通用推理能力。旨在开发一种无需微调、兼顾语义合理性与时空一致性的生成框架。

Method: 提出了 TrajGenAgent 框架，采用两阶段编排-工作器（orchestrator-worker）设计：1. 利用上下文学习（ICL）生成语义活动链；2. 通过确定性工作流（POI检索、距离感知的空间选择、动力学感知的旅行时间传播、基于LLM的持续时间估计）实现轨迹落地。同时引入了基于异常检测的评估框架。

Result: 在基准测试和大规模模拟数据集上，TrajGenAgent 在时空保真度、语义一致性及个体行为真实性方面优于现有的神经网路和LLM基线方法，同时完全避免了计算昂贵的参数更新。

Conclusion: TrajGenAgent 证明了在无需微调的情况下，通过两阶段（语义规划+确定性落地）的LLM智能体框架，能够实现高质量、高保真且兼具语义合理性的合成轨迹生成，为解决轨迹生成中的成本与隐私问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Human mobility data is important for transportation, urban planning, and epidemic control, but large-scale trajectory collection is often costly and privacy-constrained, motivating realistic synthetic trajectory generation. Existing LLM-based generators typically rely on either prompt engineering, which preserves zero-shot reasoning but lacks fine-grained spatiotemporal grounding, or trajectory-level fine-tuning, which improves statistical precision but incurs substantial computational cost and may weaken general reasoning. We propose TrajGenAgent, a semantic-aware hierarchical LLM-agent framework for human mobility trajectory generation without model fine-tuning. TrajGenAgent uses a two-stage orchestrator-worker design: an LLM first synthesizes an individual- and weekday-conditioned activity chain from historical evidence via in-context learning, and a deterministic workflow then grounds each activity into a complete visit using personalized POI retrieval, distance-aware location selection, kinematics-aware travel-time propagation, and LLM-based duration estimation. To evaluate realism beyond aggregate spatiotemporal statistics, we introduce an anomaly-detection-based evaluation framework using two complementary detectors to assess behavioral and semantic plausibility. Experiments on benchmark and large-scale simulation datasets show that TrajGenAgent improves spatiotemporal fidelity, semantic coherence, and individual-specific behavioral realism over representative neural and LLM-based baselines, while avoiding parameter updates.

</details>


### [143] [ToolSense: A Diagnostic Framework for Auditing Parametric Tool Knowledge in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12451)
*Ashutosh Hathidara,Sai Shruthi Sistla,Sebastian Schreiber,Sahil Bansal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对现有大模型工具检索基准的局限性，本文提出了ToolSense诊断框架。研究发现，现有的参数化工具检索模型在面对真实、模糊查询时性能崩塌，揭示了模型虽然能“选中”工具，但并不真正“理解”工具的知识-检索解耦问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM工具检索基准多采用冗长的完全指定查询且使用受限解码，无法真实反映模型对工具语义的理解能力，导致参数化检索模型存在潜在的评估虚高。

Method: 提出了ToolSense框架，该框架能针对任意工具目录生成三种诊断基准：真实检索基准（包含三个模糊度层级）、MCQ探测基准和QA探测基准。并用其对五种参数化模型配置进行评估。

Result: 通过ToolSense评估发现，在处理真实模糊查询时，参数化模型性能大幅下跌（跌幅约50-64%），甚至低于基准嵌入模型；此外，模型在事实探测任务上表现接近随机，证实了知识与检索能力的脱节。

Conclusion: 参数化工具检索模型存在“知识-检索解耦”现象，即模型在处理非理想化查询时表现大幅下降，且空有检索能力但缺乏对工具功能的真正理解。研究强调了构建更具挑战性和现实性基准的重要性。

Abstract: Large language models deployed as agents over large tool catalogs face a critical tool-retrieval bottleneck. As embedding-based retrieval approaches rely on compact encoders that may under-capture specialized tool semantics, parametric tool retrieval addresses this by encoding each tool as a virtual token appended to the LLM vocabulary, fine-tuned in two stages (memorization then retrieval SFT) to use the LLM as a retriever, achieving strong performance on standard ToolBench retrieval benchmarks. Yet these benchmarks use verbose, fully-specified queries, and their evaluation applies constrained decoding that restricts outputs to valid token paths, neither reveals whether the model actually understands its tools. We introduce \textbf{ToolSense}, an open-source LLM-powered diagnostic framework that takes any tool catalog as input and automatically generates three benchmarks: a Realistic Retrieval Benchmark (RRB) with queries at three ambiguity tiers, an MCQ probing benchmark, and a QA probing benchmark. Applying ToolSense to ToolBench (~47k tools) and evaluating five parametric model training configurations reveals a knowledge-retrieval dissociation: on RRB queries, several configurations collapse by ~50-64 percentage points compared to fully-specified ToolBench benchmarks, falling below the embedding-model baseline. Additionally, despite strong retrieval performance, some models score near-random on factual probes, suggesting a knowledge-retrieval dissociation. We open-source the ToolSense framework and the ToolBench diagnostic benchmarks at https://github.com/SAP/toolsense.

</details>


### [144] [Arbor: Tree Search as a Cognition Layer for Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12563)
*Neha Prakriya,Chaojun Hou,Zheng Gong,Huasha Zhao,Xi Zhao,Mou Li,Zhenyu Gu,Emad Barsoum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Arbor是一个引入结构化树搜索作为认知层的新型多智能体框架，通过明确的搜索记忆机制和监督平衡架构，实现了对复杂LLM推理全栈的跨硬件、高度稳定且优于人工优化水平的自主长期调优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有自主优化系统在处理大规模、状态相关的动作空间时，往往局限于孤立目标、无状态评估，且难以协调跨层级（应用到硬件）复杂系统优化的问题，提出一种具备长期记忆和推理能力的系统架构。

Method: 提出Arbor多智能体框架，核心设计包括：1.结构化树搜索作为认知层，维护共享的假设评分工作记忆；2.采用“编排者（Orchestrator）+领域专家（Domain Specialists）+评论家（Critic）”的监督制架构；3.将智能体能力解构为负责领域知识的“硬技能”和负责协调协议的“软技能”。

Result: Arbor在全栈LLM推理优化任务中，取得了比供应商基准高出193%的推理吞吐量-延迟帕累托改进；相比之下，单一智能体方案仅提升33%且极不稳定。同时，系统在不同硬件上表现出良好的泛化能力，且两次运行间的方差控制在2个百分点以内。

Conclusion: Arbor通过将结构化树搜索引入多智能体架构，有效地解决了自主智能体在处理大型、有状态动作空间时的复杂优化问题，证明了通过“硬技能”与“软技能”解耦以及监督者设计的协调机制，可以实现复杂系统（如LLM推理栈）的长期、稳定且超越人类专家的自动化优化。

Abstract: Arbor is a multi-agent framework that introduces structured tree search as a cognition layer for autonomous agents operating in large, stateful action spaces. Prior autonomous optimization systems operate on isolated targets with stateless evaluation. Arbor instead maintains an explicit search tree of scored hypotheses that serves as the shared working memory across agents, evolving with every measurement, treating failures as diagnostic signal that reshapes subsequent exploration, and expanding as prior successes shift the bottleneck distribution.
  We validate Arbor on full-stack LLM inference optimization, a domain where achieving peak performance has historically required coordinated effort from engineering teams across the application, framework, compiler, kernel, and hardware stack. Arbor pairs an Orchestrator agent, which drives optimization by delegating to Domain Specialists across the inference stack, with a Critic agent that safeguards stability through root-cause analysis, introspection, and measurement validation -- a checks-and-balances architecture where neither agent can unilaterally drive the system. Agent capabilities are decomposed into hard skills (domain expertise) and soft skills (coordination protocols that determine how contributions compose), enabling fully autonomous multi-day campaigns. Arbor achieves up to 193% inference throughput-latency Pareto improvement over vendor-optimized baselines, while a single agent without the harness plateaus at +33% throughput improvement and crashes irrecoverably within hours. Arbor generalizes to multiple generations of hardware platform, and run-to-run variance is within 2 percentage points demonstrating that the method is hardware-agnostic and reproducible.

</details>


### [145] [Strategic Decision Support for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12587)
*Shayan Kiyani,Sima Noorani,George Pappas,Hamed Hassani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对代理智能体（Agentic AI），本文提出了一种策略性的决策支持框架，通过在线自适应阈值和错误率约束，在保证系统可靠性的前提下，实现对辅助资源的高效调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统从辅助工具向自主代理（Agentic AI）转变，人类从决策主体变为支持者。这一角色反转使得代理的可靠性与对齐问题变得至关重要，传统的决策支持理论需重新审视以适应代理自主决策的场景。

Method: 提出一个策略性决策支持框架：将该问题建模为一个最小化支持工具调用次数的优化问题，并约束“反事实错失支持错误”（counterfactual missed-support error）。具体技术包括：导出基于支持价值的阈值策略（Threshold Rule）、无需分布假设的在线自适应阈值算法、以及用于减少不必要调用的“动态校准”（calibration-on-the-fly）方法。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在信息收集、人机协作和工具使用等多种场景下，均能稳定地控制错失支持的错误率，并能显著减少对额外支持资源的无效调用。

Conclusion: 该研究成功证明了利用决策支持理论来约束代理智能体（Agentic Systems）行为的可行性。通过将支持调用策略形式化为受限优化问题，可以在保证不超出预设错误阈值的前提下，显著降低对支持资源的依赖，为构建更可靠、更高效的智能体系统提供了通用框架。

Abstract: Traditionally, decision support studies how humans use machine learning models to make better decisions. In modern agentic systems, this division of roles is increasingly reversed: AI agents act on behalf of users, while humans and tools becomes support mechanisms around them. This role reversal brings reliability concerns to the forefront, since agentic errors can be consequential and agent behavior must remain aligned with human goals and constraints. Departing from the classical view of decision support, we revisit its two basic principles, the cost--value tradeoff of seeking support and the role of uncertainty quantification, in a setting where AI agents are the central actors. We propose a framework for strategic decision support for AI agents through an optimization problem that minimizes support usage subject to controlling a counterfactual missed-support error: the probability that the agent acts alone on instances where support would have materially improved its output. At the population level, we show that the optimal policy is a threshold rule on the value of support. Building on this structure, we develop an online algorithm that adaptively thresholds such a score and uses randomized exploration to control missed-support error without distributional assumptions. We further introduce a calibration-on-the-fly method that reduces unnecessary support calls online. We instantiate this framework across diverse scenarios, including information gathering, human--AI collaboration, and tool use, showing how each can be modeled through the same strategic decision-support lens. Experiments across these settings show that our method reliably controls the target error while substantially reducing support usage in practice.

</details>


### [146] [Pythagoras-Prover: Advancing Efficient Formal Proving via Augmented Lean Formalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12594)
*Joshua Ong Jun Leang,Zheng Zhao,Mihaela Cătălina Stoian,Qiyuan Xu,Haonan Li,Wenda Li,Shay B. Cohen,Eleonora Giunchiglia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Pythagoras-Prover 是一系列高效的 Lean 定理证明器，通过课程学习、动态数据过滤和增强形式化训练（ALF）等技术，在极低的计算成本和参数规模下，刷新了多项证明任务的性能标杆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Lean 定理证明器依赖海量计算资源（SFT与采样），导致训练和推理成本高昂，且高质量形式化证明数据稀缺。

Method: 1. 提出课程学习法（Curriculum SFT），按难度分层训练；2. 实施动态推理路径过滤，将示例压缩在8k token内；3. 引入增强形式化方法（ALF），通过自蒸馏与变体生成扩充训练集；4. 提供 autoregressive 及扩散模型两种生成路径。

Result: Pythagoras-Prover-4B 在 MiniF2F-Test 上以 4B 参数实现 86.1% pass@32，超越了 671B 参数的 DeepSeek-Prover-V2；32B 模型达到 93.0% 的 SOTA 性能，并在 PutnamBench 上表现出色。

Conclusion: Pythagoras-Prover 通过改进数据处理和训练策略，实现了极高的参数效率，证明了在有限计算预算下仍能超越大规模模型，为高效定理证明器开发提供了范式。

Abstract: Modern Lean theorem provers achieve strong performance only with substantial training and inference compute, driven in part by scarce verified proof data and the long reasoning traces of formal proof search, making both supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and sampling expensive. We introduce Pythagoras-Prover, a compute-efficient open-source family of Lean theorem provers built for practical compute budgets. The family spans two generation paradigms: autoregressive models at 4B and 32B parameters, and a first proof-of-concept diffusion-based prover (4B) that iteratively refines Lean proofs at inference time. For training efficiency, we build a Lean-verified corpus stratified into easy, medium, and hard problems for curriculum SFT, so models acquire proof skills progressively from shorter, simpler proofs to longer, harder ones. During SFT, a dynamic proof-reasoning filtering scheme preserves informative proof traces while keeping each instance within an 8k-token context budget. We also introduce Augmented Lean Formalisation (ALF), which expands scarce verified corpora into variants of formal statements, populated via self-distillation for extra training signal without formally verifying every mutated instance. By perturbing known problems while preserving their formal character, ALF reduces reliance on any statement's surface form. Empirically, Pythagoras-Prover-4B surpasses DeepSeek-Prover-V2-671B at pass@32 on MiniF2F-Test (86.1% vs 82.4%) with ~167x fewer parameters, while Pythagoras-Prover-32B sets the open-source state of the art at 93.0% on MiniF2F-Test and solves 93 of 672 PutnamBench problems. We release MiniF2F-ALF, an ALF-mutated contamination-sensitive benchmark on which every evaluated model loses accuracy; here our 32B remains strongest and our 4B matches the prior state of the art, Goedel-Prover-V2-32B.

</details>


### [147] [Reducing the Complexity of Deep Learning Models for EEG Analysis on Wearable Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12742)
*Farough Shayeste Roodi,Parham Zilouchian Moghaddam,Mahdi Mohammadi-nasab,Mehdi Modarressi,Mostafa Ersali Salehi Nasab,Masoud Daneshtalab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了通过参数量化和电极缩减技术来优化面向癫痫监测的可穿戴EEG分析模型，证明了在极小精度损耗下，通过上述方法显著降低模型计算复杂度并成功部署在资源受限设备上的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管深度神经网络在ECG和EEG信号处理方面表现优异，但可穿戴设备极度有限的计算资源、能源供应和内存带宽难以支持复杂的DNN模型，这限制了深度学习在医疗IoT领域的落地应用。

Method: 针对癫痫发作检测任务，本文使用了参数量化（Parameter Quantization）和电极减少（Electrode Reduction）两种优化方法，并对比分析了多种主流深度神经网络（DNN）模型在这些方法下的性能表现。

Result: 实验表明，通过合理应用量化和电极缩减策略，既能大幅降低DNN模型的复杂度，又能将对分类精度的影响降至最低，实现了计算效率与分析性能之间的平衡。

Conclusion: 在资源受限的可穿戴设备上部署深度学习模型是可行的。通过参数量化和电极减少技术，可以在极小精度损失的前提下，显著降低模型的计算复杂度，为可穿戴设备上的在线脑电信号分析提供了有效的优化路径。

Abstract: Wearable healthcare devices are the fastest-growing Internet of Things (IoT) sector. Many automated healthcare services rely on two crucial biological signals, namely ECG and EEG, which reflect the activity of the heart and brain, respectively. Although deep neural networks are considered the primary way to process and analyze these signals, the very tight energy and computational power constraints in wearable devices are far below the computational, energy, and memory bandwidth demands of DNN models, thereby impeding the deployment of deep learning in many practical wearable services. This paper investigates the feasibility of deploying state-of-the-art DNN models in resource-constrained wearable devices. Notably, we explore the trade-off between accuracy and computational complexity of DNNs when parameter quantization and electrode reduction methods are used. Our investigation centers on several state-of-the-art DNN models designed for EEG signal analysis, specifically for detecting epileptic seizures. Our findings demonstrate that, when applied judiciously, these techniques can significantly reduce the complexity of the DNNs under consideration with minimal adverse effects on accuracy. These results reveal the explicit trade-offs between accuracy and complexity reduction encountered when adapting DNN-based online EEG analysis for wearable devices.

</details>


### [148] [PersonaDrive: Human-Style Retrieval-Augmented VLA Agents for Closed-Loop Driving Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12616)
*Mahmoud Srewa,Praneetsai Iddamsetty,Mohammad Abdullah Al Faruque,Salma Elmalaki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PersonaDrive是一个通过检索人类专家风格化驾驶演示来条件控制VLA驾驶代理的系统，实现了无需重训即可动态切换积极、中性、保守等多种驾驶风格的高真实感闭环模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有闭环驾驶模拟中非自我（non-ego）智能体的行为单一且缺乏多样性，且现有“风格”建模多基于代理奖励（proxies）而非直接的人类指导演示。

Method: 提出了一个三阶段流水线：(i) 基于图像-文本相似度对人类驾驶数据进行离线三元组挖掘（Triplet Mining）；(ii) 训练轻量级检索头（Retrieval Head）以融合视觉特征和控制编码；(iii) 对视觉-语言-动作（VLA）骨干模型进行微调，将检索到的行为演示作为上下文进行航点预测。
