Abstract: Confidence is used for reliability, oversight, and a range of downstream decision tasks in Natural Language Processing (NLP), yet no existing method produces or evaluates a confidence for the output of a multiagent system. Prior work uses confidence within multiagent debate (MAD) to weight messages, trigger debate, or calibrate individual agents, but it never aggregates these into a single confidence for the system itself. We introduce three protocols that produce a final answer along with a single aggregated confidence by first transforming raw confidence signals to make them comparable across models, then combining them via soft voting or a probability fusion we call Bayesian fusion. This aggregated confidence is substantially more discriminative (AUARC) than that of the best single agent or the standard debate baselines, while correctness (F1-score) stays stable and recovers the losses MAD incurs on more ambiguous tasks. Analyzing two estimators, sequence probability and self-report, alongside parametric and non-parametric calibrators, we find that calibration improves F1 for both estimators while AUARC is less reliant on it. We evaluate six homogeneous and heterogeneous debating pairs per benchmark, across five benchmarks and four task types, spanning a range of model capabilities and sizes.

</details>


### [218] [Reward Modeling for Multi-Agent Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13598)
*King Yeung Tsang,Zihao Zhao,Vishal Venkataramani,Haizhou Shi,Zixuan Ke,Semih Yavuz,Shafiq Joty,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了 OrchRM，一种无需人工标注的自监督框架，通过在编排层面学习奖励模型来优化多智能体系统，在大幅提升训练效率的同时显著改善了任务执行准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于大语言模型的多智能体系统编排方法面临标注数据稀缺、计算成本高昂以及对子智能体 rollout 依赖过重等挑战。

Method: 提出 OrchRM（Orchestration Reward Modeling），这是一个无需人工标注的自监督框架。它利用多智能体执行过程中的中间产物构建“胜负对”（win-lose pairs），用于训练基于 Bradley-Terry 模型的奖励模型，从而实现直接在编排层面进行评估和优化。

Result: OrchRM 将训练效率（token 使用量）提高了 10 倍，同时将多智能体系统的测试时扩展性能（准确率）提升了高达 8%，并跨多种任务类型（数学推理、Web 问答、多跳推理）展现了通用性。

Conclusion: OrchRM 证明了基于编排层面的奖励建模是一种可扩展且高效的多智能体编排方法，能够有效提升复杂任务中的表现而无需昂贵的人工标注。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) built on Large Language Models (LLMs) require effective orchestration to coordinate specialized agents, yet training such orchestrators is hindered by limited supervision and high computational cost. We propose Orchestration Reward Modeling (OrchRM), a self-supervised framework for evaluating orchestration quality without human annotations. OrchRM leverages intermediate artifacts from multi-agent executions to construct win-lose pairs for Bradley-Terry reward model training. Unlike existing MAS test-time scaling and orchestrator training frameworks that rely on costly sub-agent rollouts, OrchRM operates directly at the orchestration level, enabling efficient and high-performing reward-guided orchestrator training and MAS test-time scaling. OrchRM improves training efficiency by up to 10x in token usage while improving MAS test-time scaling performance by up to 8% in accuracy. These gains consistently transfer across multiple domains, including mathematical reasoning, web-based question answering, and multi-hop reasoning, demonstrating orchestration-level reward modeling as a scalable direction for robust multi-agent orchestration. Code will be available at https://github.com/Wang-ML-Lab/OrchRM.

</details>


### [219] [EpiBench: Verifiable Evaluation of AI Agents on Epigenomics Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13602)
*Harihara Muralidharan,Reema Baskar,Soo Hee Lee,Tim Proctor,Kenny Workman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EpiBench是一个专注于短程表观基因组分析的验证性基准，测试发现当前AI智能体仍无法 reliably 完成复杂的生物学分析任务，主要瓶颈在于缺乏针对特定生物实验的科学判断力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决当前表观基因组学分析中缺乏系统性、可验证的自动化评估基准的问题，评估智能体在该领域的实际执行与判断能力。

Method: 构建了包含106项评估的EpiBench基准测试，覆盖CUT&Tag/CUT&RUN、ATAC-seq、ChIP-seq和DNA甲基化工作流程，评估智能体在真实工作流状态下做出明确分析决策及输出可验证结果的能力。

Result: 在5,088条测试轨迹中，没有系统能通过大多数测试（最高通过率仅为45.0%）。GPT-5.5/Pi 表现最佳，但整体能力仍有显著的科学判断短板。

Conclusion: 目前的LLM智能体在表观基因组学分析任务中表现不及格，大多能处理基础文件操作和中间计算，但在需要深入、特定实验逻辑的科学判断时表现乏力。

Abstract: We introduce EpiBench, a verifiable benchmark for short-horizon epigenomics analysis. EpiBench evaluates whether agents can make well-defined analysis decisions from realistic workflow states and return deterministically gradable answers. The benchmark includes 106 evaluations across CUT\&Tag/CUT\&RUN, ATAC-seq, ChIP-seq, and DNA methylation workflows. Across 5,088 valid trajectories from 16 model-harness pairs, no system passed a majority of attempts: GPT-5.5 / Pi led at 45.0\% (143/318 attempts; 95\% confidence interval (CI), 36.3--53.7), followed by GPT-5.5 / OpenAI Codex at 39.9\% (127/318 attempts; 95\% CI, 31.6--48.3). Claude Opus 4.8 Max / Pi and GPT-5.4 / Pi each passed 39.0\% (124/318 attempts; 95\% CI, 30.2--47.8 and 31.0--47.0, respectively). Performance varies across assay types, and many failed runs still contain parts of the correct answer. Agents often found the right files and computed useful intermediate results, but failed when the task required deeper, assay-specific scientific judgment.

</details>


### [220] [Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning from Delayed Marketplace Feedback for Objective-Weight Adaptation in Three-Sided Dispatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13604)
*Haochen Wu,Yi Hou,Shiguang Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DoorDash提出了一种基于离线强化学习的调度权重优化系统，该系统在不替换现有组合优化器的前提下，通过学习权重参数动态平衡物流配送中的效率与质量，并已在实际生产中验证了其有效性与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三方市场（如外卖平台）的调度决策受复杂、延迟的运营指标（配送速度、人力利用率、商家拥堵）影响，需要一种能在保证生产约束的前提下，从海量历史数据中学习并动态调整调度目标权重的方法。

Method: 提出了一种基于店铺层级的离线强化学习策略，通过离线训练的策略选择离散权重系数（Multiplier）来干预现有的组合优化分配器，而非直接替换；采用共享价值函数、Double Q-learning目标及保守正则化技术处理噪声、延迟和耦合反馈。

Result: 在DoorDash的生产环境切换实验（switchback experiment）中，该系统成功提高了打包效率并降低了配送员端的成本，同时未对用户端交付质量产生负面影响。

Conclusion: 在不牺牲交付质量的前提下，通过离线强化学习动态调整调度权重，可有效平衡三方市场中的配送效率与成本，验证了在复杂物流系统中利用世界反馈进行安全策略优化的可行性。

Abstract: Dispatch in three-sided marketplaces provides a natural setting for reinforcement learning from world feedback: decisions are evaluated by delayed operational outcomes such as delivery speed, courier utilization, and merchant congestion. We present a deployed reinforcement learning system at DoorDash that adapts dispatch objective weights in a large-scale food-delivery marketplace using delayed signals. Rather than replacing the combinatorial assignment optimizer, a store-level policy learned from logged marketplace data selects a discrete multiplier that shifts the dispatch optimizer's tradeoff between delivery quality and batching efficiency. This interface enables offline policy learning under noisy, delayed, and coupled feedback while preserving production feasibility constraints and operational safeguards. We train a shared value function using centralized offline data and decentralized store-level execution, with Double Q-learning targets and a conservative regularizer to reduce out-of-distribution value overestimation. In a production switchback experiment, the offline-trained policy increases batching and reduces courier-side time costs without degrading customer-facing delivery quality. Results illustrate how world feedback from a live economic and logistics system can be used to safely adapt decision policies online.

</details>


### [221] [Reasoning as Pattern Matching: Shared Mechanisms in Human and LLM Everyday Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13607)
*Zach Studdiford,Gary Lupyan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 这项研究揭示了人类在日常因果推理中与大语言模型一样，更多地依赖“模式匹配”而非严格的“抽象逻辑推理”，挑战了人类思维具备高等抽象模型优越性的传统假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨究竟是大语言模型（LLM）因缺乏抽象世界模型而导致推理错误，还是人类的日常推理本身就并非基于抽象模型，而是基于模式匹配。

Method: 通过对比实验评估25个LLM与人类在日常常识推理任务中的表现；利用注意力头（Attention heads）追踪大模型的推理机制，并分析其如何重现人类的推理错误。

Result: 研究发现人类与LLM在面对无关细节干扰时，会产生相似的推理错误；模型中的模式匹配机制能够有效预测人类在特定提示下的“莫名其妙”的推理偏差。

Conclusion: 人类的日常因果推理与大语言模型（LLMs）的行为模式具有高度一致性，两者主要通过模式匹配（Pattern-matching）而非抽象世界模型（Abstract world models）来驱动。

Abstract: When large language models (LLMs) fail to generalize or make haphazard errors in reasoning, it is often taken as evidence that LLMs are not truly reasoning, but rather performing a kind of pattern matching. The implication is that people's behavior does not exhibit the same types of failures because human reasoning uses principled and abstract world models. We evaluate human participants and 25 LLMs on their ability to engage in common-sense reasoning about a variety of everyday situations and observe similar patterns of errors in both people and models. We then identify the set of attention heads driving LLM responses and find that these heads implement a form of pattern-matching. These attention heads allow us to predict seemingly inexplicable reasoning errors in people caused by ostensibly irrelevant prompt details. Taken together, our results suggest that everyday causal reasoning in people and LLMs is more consistent with a form of pattern-matching than with abstract world models.

</details>


### [222] [AgentBeats: Agentifying Agent Assessment for Openness, Standardization, and Reproducibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13608)
*Xiaoyuan Liu,Jianhong Tu,Yuqi Chen,Siyuan Xie,Sihan Ren,Tianneng Shi,Gal Gantar,Evan Sandoval,Donghyun Lee,Daniel Miao,Peter J. Gilbert,Nick Hynes,Mauro Staver,Warren He,David Marn,Andrew Low,Xi Zhang,Elron Bandel,Michal Shmueli-Scheuer,Siva Reddy,Alexandre Drouin,Alexandre Lacoste,Ramayya Krishnan,Elham Tabassi,Yu Su,Victor Barres,Chenguang Wang,Wenbo Guo,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对现有代理评估环境高度碎片化的问题，本文提出了“代理化代理评估”（AAA）框架及其实作AgentBeats。该框架通过标准化通信协议实现了评估逻辑与代理设计的解耦，实验证明其在大规模应用下具备良好的兼容性、保真度和可重复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理评估系统存在高度碎片化、严重依赖特定集成、测试-生产环境不匹配以及缺乏统一评估协议等问题，导致公平比较困难。需要一个开放的、代理无关的标准化评估接口。

Method: 提出“代理化代理评估”（AAA）架构，利用标准化协议A2A（任务管理）和MCP（工具访问）实现评估逻辑与代理实现的解耦。并推出了AgentBeats框架，定义了五种实用操作模式以适应不同评估需求。

Result: 通过五个月的开放竞赛（298个评判代理和467个被测代理）和代码代理案例研究，证明了AAA框架具有高覆盖率、实用性和保真度，并能够揭示以往评估方法缺失的对比洞察。

Conclusion: AAA（代理化代理评估）通过统一所有参与者的交互协议（A2A和MCP），成功构建了一个解耦、通用且可扩展的评估框架。AgentBeats作为其实现方案，证明了该方法在处理现实世界约束（如隐私、开放性）和大规模应用中的有效性。

Abstract: Agent systems are advancing quickly across domains, but their evaluation remains fragmented. Most benchmarks rely on fixed, LLM-centric harnesses that require heavy integration, create test-production mismatch, and limit fair comparison across diverse agent designs. The root problem is the lack of an open, agent-agnostic assessment interface. We advocate Agentified Agent Assessment (AAA), where evaluation is performed by judge agents and all participants interact through standardized protocols: A2A for task management and MCP for tool access. Conventional benchmarking defines two separate interfaces, one for the benchmark and one for the agent, while AAA only needs one; this yields a generic, unified framework that separates assessment logic from agent implementation and enables reproducible, interoperable, and multi-agent evaluation. We further introduce AgentBeats as a concrete realization of AAA: we identify five practical operation modes that make standardized assessment compatible with real-world constraints on openness, privacy, and reproducibility.
  To evaluate our design at scale, we conduct two studies: a five-month open competition that drew 298 judge agents across 12 categories together with 467 subject agents from independent participants, showing that AAA applies across a heterogeneous range of benchmarks; and a case study on coding agents that confirms agentified evaluation preserves fidelity with the public record while surfacing previously missing head-to-head results, yielding research insights about agent design. Combining a community-scale field study and a controlled coding case study, we verify that AAA delivers coverage, practicality, and fidelity across heterogeneous scenarios at scale. Together, AAA and AgentBeats offer a clear path toward open, standardized, and reproducible agent assessment.

</details>


### [223] [Beyond Runtime Enforcement: Shield Synthesis as Defensibility Analysis for Adversarial Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13621)
*Achraf Hsain,Sultan Almuhammadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张将屏蔽强化学习从“运行时安全约束工具”转型为“设计时系统架构分析工具”，通过博弈论与强化学习的结合，为网络防御提供正式的可防御性认证（defensibility verdict）与定量评估指纹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者认为现有的屏蔽强化学习（Shielded RL）将自动化机制仅作为运行时安全约束的做法是“错误的产物”，忽视了该机制在系统架构设计阶段作为分析与验证工具的巨大潜力。

Method: 提出了一种基于约束双人安全博弈（two-player safety game）的分析框架，将屏蔽机制转化为网络防御的分析工具；结合吸引子计算（attractor computation）与屏蔽约束下的对抗性多智能体强化学习，提取拓扑层面的可防御性指标与指纹。

Result: 实验表明 formal 可防御性与 operational 有效性是两个互补的独立维度；小规模架构变更可能剧烈改变操作表现，但对形式化安全性边界影响甚微。研究成功构建了区分这两种维度的可防御性指纹。

Conclusion: 屏蔽（Shielding）机制的价值不应局限于运行时约束，而应作为一种“设计时”的分析工具。通过定义“可防御性指纹”（defensibility fingerprint），该方法能够为复杂的拓扑架构决策提供正式的安全性与操作表现洞察，而非仅仅生成一个受限的策略。

Abstract: Shielded reinforcement learning is typically presented as a runtime safety mechanism that compiles temporal-logic specifications into automata restricting an agent's actions. We argue this is the wrong product. The same automata-theoretic machinery -- specification compilation, product game construction, attractor computation, and winning-region extraction -- is better read as a design-time analytical instrument whose outputs are structural insights about a system rather than runtime constraints on a deployed agent.
  We instantiate this through a constrained two-player safety game for network defense. The two specifications are enforced asymmetrically: the defender specification defines the unsafe region of the game, whereas the attacker specification restricts the adversary's legal actions during attractor computation. Solving the game yields a defensibility verdict -- a formal certificate that a topology-specification pair is or is not defensible -- with the associated winning region and shield.
  Beyond the binary verdict, we derive topology-level metrics from the attractor structure and combine them with post-convergence behavior from shield-constrained adversarial multi-agent reinforcement learning. Together these form a defensibility fingerprint capturing both a network's formal safety properties and its operational behavior under adaptive play.
  A what-if analysis shows that formal defensibility and operational effectiveness capture distinct aspects of security: small architectural changes can produce large shifts in operational outcomes while leaving formal safety margins nearly unchanged. Shield synthesis is thus most valuable not as a deployment mechanism for safe agents, but as a framework for answering architectural questions about whether, where, and how a system can be defended. The defensibility verdict is the output, not the safe policy.

</details>


### [224] [Before You Think: System 0, AI-Mediated Cognition and Cognitive Colonization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13658)
*Marianna Bergamaschi Ganapini,Massimo Chiriatti,Enrico Panai,Giuseppe Riva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析了AI对认知影响的三种框架，指出“系统0”具有独特地位，并提出“认知殖民”概念，揭示了AI如何通过不可见的架构设计潜移默化地重塑人类认知，呼吁关注其带来的深层哲学与实践风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有框架未能充分解释人工智能如何通过架构层面的嵌入，在用户难以察觉的情况下植入外部利益，因此需要通过“认知殖民”的概念来揭示AI对人类认知结构隐蔽而深远的影响。

Method: 比较分析法。通过对比分析“三系统理论（Tri-System Theory）”、“Thinkframes”和“系统0（System 0）”三种框架，探讨AI对个体推理和集体认知实践的影响。

Result: 区分了现有框架的局限性，并提出了“认知殖民（cognitive colonization）”概念，强调AI系统在架构设计上对人类思维模式的潜移默化及操控。

Conclusion: AI系统通过“认知殖民”对人类认知构成了难以察觉的深层结构性影响；“系统0”概念在解释这种潜移默化的影响方面具有独特的理论价值，超越了现有的三系统理论和Thinkframes框架。

Abstract: This paper examines three recent frameworks for understanding the cognitive and epistemic consequences of artificial intelligence: Tri-System Theory, Thinkframes, and System 0. It argues that while the first two capture important dimensions of AI's influence on individual reasoning and collective epistemic practices, System 0 occupies a theoretically distinctive position that neither can fully replicate. The paper introduces the concept of cognitive colonization, according to which AI systems can embed external interests within the architecture of the self in ways that are difficult for users to perceive. Because such systems are already widely deployed, understanding these invisible forms of influence is an urgent philosophical and practical task.

</details>


### [225] [EurekAgent: Agent Environment Engineering is All You Need For Autonomous Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13662)
*Amy Xin,Jiening Siow,Junjie Wang,Zijun Yao,Fanjin Zhang,Jian Song,Lei Hou,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究认为自主科学发现的瓶颈在于环境而非模型，提出了“环境工程”理念并开发了EurekAgent，通过规范化环境设计显著提升了科研代理的产出效率与成本效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM能力的提升，自主科学发现的瓶颈已从“代理工作流设计”转移到“代理环境设计”，需要通过优化环境来增强探索与协作，并抑制由于奖励扣分等带来的负面行为。

Method: 提出了EurekAgent架构，通过四个维度进行环境工程：1. 权限工程（安全执行与隔离评估）；2. 工件工程（基于文件系统和Git的协作）；3. 预算工程（成本感知探索）；4. 人机协同工程（简化监督与干预）。

Result: EurekAgent在数学、内核工程和机器学习任务中达到SOTA水平，特别是在26-圆堆叠问题中以不到11美元的API成本发现了新的最优解。

Conclusion: 环境工程（Environment Engineering）是推动自主科学发现的关键，通过系统构建和优化代理运行环境，可以有效提升LLM代理在科学研究中的产出能力与可靠性。

Abstract: LLM-based agents have shown increasing potential in automating scientific discovery. Given an optimizable metric and an execution environment, they can propose, validate, and iterate scientific solutions, and have produced results that outperform human-designed approaches. As model capabilities continue to improve, we argue that the bottleneck for autonomous scientific discovery is shifting from prescribing agent workflows to designing agent environments: the resources, constraints, and interfaces that shape agent behavior. We frame this as environment engineering: building environments that amplify productive behaviors, such as open-ended exploration, systematic artifact management, and inter-agent collaboration, while suppressing harmful behaviors, such as reward hacking and high-friction human oversight. We present EurekAgent, an environment-engineered agent system for metric-driven autonomous scientific discovery. EurekAgent engineers the environment along four dimensions: permissions engineering for bounded agent execution and isolated evaluation; artifact engineering for filesystem and Git-based collaboration; budget engineering for budget-aware exploration; and human-in-the-loop engineering for easy human supervision and intervention. EurekAgent sets new state-of-the-art results on multiple mathematics, kernel engineering, and machine learning tasks, including new state-of-the-art 26-circle packing results discovered with less than $11 in total API cost. We open-source our code and results, and call for environment engineering as a core research direction for developing reliable autonomous research agents.

</details>


### [226] [Agents-K1: Towards Agent-native Knowledge Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13669)
*Zongsheng Cao,Bihao Zhan,Jinxin Shi,Jiong Wang,Fangchen Yu,Zhijie Zhong,Zijie Guo,Tianshuo Peng,Zhuo Liu,Yi Xie,Xiang Zhuang,Yue Fan,Runmin Ma,Shiyang Feng,Xiangchao Yan,Anran Liu,Peng Ye,Wenlong Zhang,Shufei Zhang,Chunfeng Song,Fenghua Ling,Jie Zhou,Liang He,Bo Zhang,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Agents-K1 是一个端到端的科学知识编排系统，旨在通过构建包含细粒度论文信息的科学知识图谱（Scholar-KG），提升 AI Agent 处理复杂科学推理和信息检索的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 研究智能体侧重于任务编排而忽视了科学知识编排，且常将论文简化为摘要或简单的引用关系，缺乏对论文核心（如实体、主张、证据、机制及方法演变）的深度解析，导致科学推理能力受限。

Method: 1. 提出 Agents-K1 端到端流水线：包括多模态解析器（提取实体、多模态证据和引用关系等）、基于 4B 参数的信息抽取后端（使用 GRPO 算法和规则奖励训练）、以及 GraphAnything CLI 接口（整合网页搜索、图检索与跨文档遍历）。
2. 构建并发布 Scholar-KG：处理了 246 万篇科学论文，并开源了一百万篇论文的子集。

Result: Agents-K1 在科学信息提取、大规模知识图谱构建以及多跳科学推理任务中表现出优于现有方法的性能。

Conclusion: Agents-K1 通过构建由细粒度科学知识构成的 Scholar-KG，有效解决了现有 LLM 研究智能体在科学知识编排方面的不足，证明了端到端知识图谱构建与高效检索对于科学逻辑推理的重要性。

Abstract: Current LLM-based research agents have advanced through agent orchestration, yet largely overlook scientific knowledge orchestration. Existing works often reduce papers to abstracts, surface mentions, and flat \texttt{cites} edges, omitting key entities, claims, evidence, mechanisms, and method lineages essential for scientific reasoning. To this end, we introduce \textbf{Agents-K1}, an end-to-end knowledge orchestration pipeline that converts raw documents into agent-native scientific knowledge graphs. Agents-K1 integrates three components under a unifying theoretical foundation: a multimodal parser whose five-module schema captures entities, multimodal evidence, citations, and typed inter-entity relations across the full paper rather than abstracts alone; a 4B information-extraction backbone trained with GRPO under a rule-based reward; and a graphanything CLI, a tri-source agent interface that unifies web search, multimodal graph retrieval, and cross-document traversal. On top of this, we process 2.46 million scientific papers across six subjects to produce \textbf{Scholar-KG}, of which we release a one-million-paper subset, and the full Scholar-KG is accessible via the SCP link below. The same pipeline can be extended to general-domain corpora and to schema-conformant data synthesis. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Agents-K1 achieves superior performance in scientific information extraction, knowledge graph construction, and multi-hop scientific reasoning.

</details>


### [227] [Automated reproducibility assessments in the social and behavioral sciences using large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13670)
*Tobias Holtdirk,Pietro Marcolongo,Anna Steinberg Schulten,Felix Henninger,Stefan Rose,Sarah Ball,Bolei Ma,Frauke Kreuter,Markus Weinmann,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究表明，大语言模型在评估社科领域研究的可重复性方面表现出色，其在效应量恢复和结论一致性方面均优于人类重新分析，证明了自动化可重复性审计的可行性与规模化潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的社会和行为科学可重复性评估主要依赖研究人员手动重新分析数据，这种方法资源消耗巨大且难以规模化。研究旨在探究大语言模型是否能实现这一过程的自动化，以提高审计效率。

Method: 研究人员构建了一个自动化LLM管线，针对社会和行为科学领域76项已发表且具有预定义主张的研究进行分析。将LLM生成的分析结果与原始研究结果及人类专家再分析的结果进行对比，评估指标包括效应量（Cohen's d，容差±0.05）及定性结论的一致性。

Result: 在排除7项LLM无法生成有效效应量的研究后，LLM在41%的研究中成功恢复了原始效应量（人类再分析为34%）；在定性结论一致性方面，LLM达到了96%的一致性，而人类再分析仅为74%。

Conclusion: 大语言模型（LLMs）能够作为一种可扩展的工具，用于社会和行为科学领域实证研究结果的自动化可重复性评估，为系统化审计奠定了基础。

Abstract: Reproducibility in the social and behavioral sciences is typically evaluated by independent researchers who reanalyze the original data to assess whether the published findings can be recovered. However, such approaches are resource-intensive and difficult to scale. Here, we show that large language models (LLMs) can automate reproducibility assessments. Using N=76 published studies with predefined claims from the behavioral and social sciences, we compare LLM-generated analysis with the original findings and human reanalysis. For 7 studies, the LLM could not produce a viable effect size estimate. For the remaining studies, our LLM pipeline recovered the original effect sizes in 41% of studies using a +/-0.05 tolerance in Cohen's d. Further, our LLM pipeline reached the same qualitative conclusion as the original study in 96% of cases, where conclusions indicate whether the reanalysis supports the original claim. For comparison, human reanalysts recovered the original effect sizes in 34% of studies and reached the same qualitative conclusion in 74% of cases. Together, these results show that LLMs can serve as a scalable tool for automated reproducibility assessment and provide a foundation for systematic auditing of empirical results in the social and behavioral sciences.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [228] [Random Proposals: A Softmax-Based Local-Improvement Framework for Maximum Weighted Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12692)
*Ahmed M. Alzuhair,Ahmed Alherz*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于最大权匹配的随机局部改进算法，通过Softmax采样实现了 $\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon$ 的近似比，并具备准线性的运行效率，支持收敛速度与精度之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决最大权匹配（MWM）问题中计算复杂性与近似精度之间的平衡问题，寻求一种具有理论收敛保证且运行高效的局部改进方法。

Method: 提出了一种基于Softmax偏差采样的随机局部改进算法（Randomized Local-Improvement Algorithm），通过局部 $\varepsilon$-占优机制进行匹配更新。

Result: 算法实现了期望 $\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon$ 的近似比，其时间复杂度为 $O(m\log(1/\varepsilon)/p_{\min})$，在特定条件下可简化为准线性时间 $O(m\log(1/\varepsilon))$。

Conclusion: 该算法为最大权匹配问题提供了一种灵活且高效的近似方案，通过引入Softmax偏差采样机制，在保持计算效率的同时实现了理论上的近似保证，并允许在收敛速度和近似精度之间进行动态权衡。

Abstract: We propose a randomized local-improvement algorithm for the Maximum Weighted Matching (MWM) problem. Our method introduces a softmax-based biased sampling mechanism that achieves local $\varepsilon$-dominance and yields an expected $\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon$ approximation ratio. We prove convergence guarantees and show that the algorithm runs in $O\!\left(m\log(1/\varepsilon)/p_{\min}\right)$ time, where $p_{\min}$ is the minimum softmax proposal probability over all edges; under mild conditions on the bias parameter and weight range, this simplifies to $O(m\log(1/\varepsilon))$. The framework provides a tunable tradeoff between convergence speed and approximation quality.

</details>


### [229] [A unified complexity bound for logconcave sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12694)
*Yunbum Kook,Santosh S. Vempala*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 利用In-and-Out算法与指数提升技术，为任意对数凹分布的抽样提供了一种兼顾简单性与紧凑性（nearly tight）的统一收敛性分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对数凹分布抽样算法的收敛速度分析在约束条件和良构条件（well-conditioned）下不够统一或紧凑，缺乏一个简单的、普适的理论框架。

Method: 采用In-and-Out算法与指数提升（exponential lifting）技术，并推导出了提升分布的更优Poincaré常数限制。

Result: 该算法在约束设置（如限制在凸体内的高斯分布）和良构设置（如强对数凹和光滑密度）下，均实现了近乎最优的收敛速度。

Conclusion: 通过引入指数提升（exponential lifting）及对提升分布Poincaré常数的改进界，提供了一种抽样任意对数凹分布的通用、简洁且近乎紧（nearly tight）的分析框架。

Abstract: We give a simple, unified, and nearly tight bound for sampling arbitrary logconcave distributions from a warm start using the In-and-Out algorithm along with exponential lifting. The main new ingredient in the analysis is an improved bound on the Poincaré constant of a lifted distribution. As a consequence, the resulting convergence rate is nearly tight for both constrained settings (e.g., Gaussian restricted to a convex body) and well-conditioned settings (e.g., strongly logconcave and smooth densities).

</details>


### [230] [Diffusion-Network Alignment: An Efficient Algorithm and Explicit Probability Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12879)
*Ziao Wang,Lei Ying*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 针对信息不对称的扩散树与社交网络匹配问题，提出了一种基于树相关性的高效算法。该方法在稀疏图上展现了极高的匹配精度，并证明了顶点的匹配可靠性随其靠近扩散树根部而显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统网络匹配问题假设两个网络均为全观测状态，而现实场景（如通信追踪或接触者追踪）中存在显著的信息不对称。因此，研究者旨在开发一种能处理非对称扩散轨迹与目标社交网络之间匹配的新方法。

Method: 提出了一种基于“树相关性测试”（tree correlation tests）的高效算法，旨在从局部邻域中提取特征以解决扩散树（Diffusion Tree）到网络（Network）的匹配问题，并涵盖了信息不对称场景。

Result: 在稀疏图状态下，该算法能以极高概率实现正确的顶点匹配。研究进一步推导出了每个顶点匹配成功概率的显式下界，并证实这些下界与顶点在扩散树中的深度紧密相关。

Conclusion: 该研究通过树相关性测试通过邻域结构提取信息，成功实现了扩散树与网络之间的匹配。研究证明了该算法在稀疏图中具有很高的匹配准确性，并揭示了匹配成功概率与顶点深度之间的正相关规律：越靠近根节点的顶点，匹配成功的概率越高。

Abstract: This paper studies a variation of the classic network alignment problem, named diffusion-network alignment. The goal is to align the vertices of a rooted diffusion tree to the vertices of a network, where the diffusion tree could be from a communication trace or contact tracing, and the network could be an online or offline social network. Different from the classic network alignment where both networks are fully observed, this model captures the information asymmetry of two networks. To solve this problem, this paper presents an efficient algorithm based on tree correlation tests to extract alignment information from local neighborhoods. We analyze the performance of the algorithm in the sparse graph regime and show that with high probability, all matched pairs are correct. Furthermore, for each vertex on the diffusion tree, this paper establishes an explicit lower bound on the probability that the vertex is correctly matched. These lower bounds are depth-dependent and increase as vertices get closer to the root.

</details>


### [231] [Binary Search Variants: A Comprehensive Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12970)
*Ali Dasdan*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文针对二分查找实现易错的问题，通过统一的循环不变式、Dafny 形式化验证和统一化算法（bsearch_ultimate），提供了一套经过 9500+ 用例验证的二分查找实现规范和记忆法则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二分查找虽逻辑简单，但在实际实现中极易出错（例如边界条件导致的死循环或错误结果），缺乏统一的理论分析和健壮的实现规范。

Method: 1. 定义五种核心二分查找变体及六种派生查询函数；2. 统一使用循环不变式进行分析；3. 提供 Python 实现、Dafny 形式化证明及伪代码；4. 引入 `bsearch_ultimate` 统一接口；5. 通过 9500+ 测试用例及 Dafny 验证集保证正确性。

Result: 1. 提出了一个能涵盖所有变体的统一搜索算法 `bsearch_ultimate`；2. 建立了边界选择与循环条件、更新公式之间的记忆法则；3. 完成了包含 9500+ 测试和 21 项形式化验证的健壮实现，并成功定位了典型 bug。

Conclusion: 二分查找的实现复杂性源于边界条件处理，通过统一的循环不变式和规则化方法，可以实现不仅正确且易于验证的算法。引入 `bsearch_ultimate` 能够涵盖多种变体，提升代码的可维护性。

Abstract: Binary search is deceptively simple in concept yet notoriously difficult to implement correctly. This paper presents a unified treatment of binary search: five core variants, six derived query functions, and four standard library implementations (BSD, glibc, Java, C++ STL), each with consistent notation, loop invariants, and analysis. We introduce bsearch_ultimate, a combined search that subsumes all variants in a single call. Every algorithm is provided as synchronized Python code, Dafny formal proof, and pseudocode. All implementations are validated by over 9,500 tests and 21 Dafny formal verifications; an additional six deliberately faulty implementations demonstrate common bug categories and Dafny's ability to detect them. We also provide memorable rules linking boundary choices to loop conditions and update formulas.

</details>


### [232] [Learning-Augmented Approximation for Unrelated-Machines Makespan Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13133)
*Kaito Baba,Evripidis Bampis,Giorgos Mitropoulos*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文拓展了 Antoniadis 等人的学习增强框架，成功应用于 $R\|C_{\max}$ 调度问题。通过预测重作业分配，算法在预测准确时可达到接近最优解的近似度，且能确保预测失效时的稳健性（最坏情况 2-近似）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Antoniadis et al. (ICLR 2025) 提出了一种针对 NP-hard 选择问题的学习增强框架，但该框架在其他重要领域（如调度问题）的适用性尚不明确，本文旨在解决这一开放问题。

Method: 利用“重作业分配（heavy job assignments）”预测作为辅助信息，设计了一种学习增强算法，该算法在预测准确时能实现$(1+\varepsilon)$-近似，而在预测完全错误时的最坏情况性能为2-近似。

Result: 针对无关机机（unrelated machines）的最小化最大完工时间（makespan minimization）问题，通过理论推导证明了所提算法的性能，并通过实验验证了该方法的可行性与有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究成功将学习增强算法框架从选择问题拓展至调度问题（$R\|C_{\max}$），证明了在存在预测误差的情况下该方法仍能保持较好的近似性能，并验证了其在实际场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Recently, Antoniadis et al. (ICLR 2025) proposed a framework for incorporating predictions to approximate NP-hard selection problems. Despite its simplicity, this approach tightly matches theoretical lower bounds, making its generalization highly compelling. We address an open question raised in the work of Antoniadis et al., concerning the extension of this approach to other important problems outside the class of selection problems, such as scheduling. We develop a learning-augmented algorithm for the makespan minimization problem on unrelated machines, denoted by $R\|C_{\max}$. By using predictions of heavy job assignments, we achieve a polynomial-time $(1+\varepsilon)$-approximation for accurate predictions that smoothly degrades to a worst-case 2-approximation as the error increases. We conclude our work with an empirical analysis of our method.

</details>


### [233] [Random Generation of $k$-coloured Motzkin Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13151)
*Elena Barcucci,Antonio Bernini,Stefano Bilotta,Renzo Pinzani*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了k-染色Motzkin路径的解析与组合特性，揭示了其与奇数高度前缀数的关联，并提出了一种高效的线性时间随机生成算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究k-染色Motzkin路径（水平步有k种颜色）的计数性质，并探索其与特定路径统计量（在奇数高度结束的前缀数）之间的内在联系。

Method: 采用分析组合学（Analytical Combinatorics）方法进行理论探讨；利用组合双射或递归结构进行组合推导；基于生成的组合性质设计线性时间随机抽样算法。

Result: 建立了k-染色Motzkin路径与指定高度前缀数之间的数学联系，并成功推导出一种针对该路径的线性时间随机生成算法。

Conclusion: 通过分析k-染色Motzkin路径与在奇数高度结束的前缀数量之间的联系，证明了该类路径具有良好的组合性质，并据此设计了线性时间的随机生成算法。

Abstract: We study k-coloured Motzkin paths, namely Motzkin paths in which horizontal steps can be coloured in k different ways, and investigate their connection with the number of prefixes ending at odd height from both an analytical and a combinatorial point of view. Moreover, the combinatorial approach provides a random generation algorithm for k-coloured Motzkin paths in linear-time.

</details>


### [234] [(Un)ranking Permutation Classes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13160)
*Nathanaël Hassler,Vincent Vajnovszki*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文针对避开长度为3的模式的排列，提出了一套在字典序和逆字典序下进行排名（ranking）与反排名（unranking）的高效算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然避开长度为3的模式的排列计数问题已被Catalan数解决，但在算法层面，如何高效地对这些排列进行排名（ranking）和反排名（unranking）仍需专门的方法。

Method: 利用Catalan组合结构特性，结合组合数学中的计数方法，设计了针对特定避空模式排列的排序（由排列映射至秩）与反排序（由秩映射至排列）算法。

Result: 实现了针对此类排列的字典序与逆字典序下的秩计算映射及其逆映射，优化了此类受限排列的生成与访问效率。

Conclusion: 该研究成功提出了针对避开长度为3的模式的排列进行字典序和逆字典序排序（Ranking）与反排序（Unranking）的高效算法。

Abstract: Permutations avoiding a pattern of length three are enumerated by the Catalan numbers. In this work, we present methods for ranking and unranking such permutations in lexicographic or colexicographic order.

</details>


### [235] [Sketching Intersection Profiles: A Simple Proof and Three Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13182)
*Flavio Chierichetti,Mirko Giacchini,Ravi Kumar,Alessandro Panconesi,Erasmo Tani,Andrew Tomkins*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了三个草图问题，证明了顶点邻域大小、覆盖函数估计及随机效用模型在 $\ell_\infty$-范数下的草图复杂度均为 $\tilde{\Theta}(n^2)$，并通过连接到“相交轮廓”草图问题统一了这些下界证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对图顶点邻域大小、覆盖函数以及随机效用模型（RUM）等问题的草图复杂度缺乏紧凑的下界界定，作者旨在通过统一的框架解决这些问题的复杂度难题。

Method: 1. 建立与“相交轮廓”草图问题（计算 $T \cap S \neq \varnothing$ 的概率）的连接；2. 利用已有关于数据库项集频率估计问题的下界结论进行推导；3. 提供了一种基于基本概率论的简化替代证明方法，用于常数精度下的下界分析。

Result: 1. 顶点邻域大小草图需要 $\Omega(n^2)$ 比特；2. 覆盖函数的草图空间复杂度为 $\tilde{\Theta}(n^2)$；3. $\ell_\infty$-范数下的随机效用模型草图存在 $\Omega(n^2)$ 的下界，改进了原有的 $\Omega(n \log n)$ 结果。

Conclusion: 该研究确定了三个草图问题（Sketching Problems）的复杂度，证明了它们均需要 $\Omega(n^2)$ 比特的空间复杂度，并指出这些边界与“相交轮廓”（intersection profile）草图问题之间的内在联系。

Abstract: In this work we settle the complexity of three sketching problems. (i) We show that sketching vertex neighborhood sizes in graphs requires $Ω(n^2)$ bits, standing in sharp contrast to the $\tilde{O}(n)$ complexity of sketching edge cuts. (ii) We obtain tight lower and upper bounds of $\tildeΘ(n^2)$ for sketching coverage functions with additive and multiplicative errors. (iii) We prove an $Ω(n^2)$ lower bound for sketching Random Utility Models under the $\ell_\infty$-norm, improving upon the previous $Ω(n \log n)$ bound and matching a known upper bound to within logarithmic factors.
  These bounds are obtained through a connection with the problem of sketching the intersection profile of a distribution $D$ on $2^{[n]}$. Specifically, we seek a succinct data structure that, for any query set $S \subseteq [n]$, approximates the quantity $\Pr_{T \sim D}[T \cap S \neq \varnothing]$ to within a small constant additive error. One can obtain lower bounds for this latter problem directly from known results about the itemset frequency estimation problem in databases for which tight bounds are known. As an additional contribution, we also provide an alternative proof for the intersection profile sketching lower bound, in the setting in which the accuracy parameter is constant. This proof relies solely on elementary probability avoiding the heavier machinery used in previous proofs.

</details>


### [236] [Split Tallies: A Discrete Certificate Calculus for Auditing Dynamic Ordered Sets in Constant Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13272)
*Faruk Alpay,Levent Sarioglu*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该文提出了一种高效的有序集回溯审计方案，仅需极小的存储开销即可实现近乎完美的欺诈检测，支持多种动态操作并保证了O(log n)的操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在不可信存储环境下，如何以较低的存储和计算开销，对动态有序集（如插入、删除、查询等操作）进行事后审计，以确保操作结果的正确性和一致性。

Method: 利用“最大间隙（maximal gaps）”表示顺序语义，通过四阶段（出生、引用、消耗、时间戳）管理间隙生命周期。结合两个隐藏字段累加器（Hidden Field Accumulators）验证账本一致性。使用叶子节点导向的(2,4)-树实现维护者，支持可审计的批量更新。

Result: 1. 该方案对于诚实执行的审计接受率为1；2. 对于错误操作，在不受计算限制的攻击者面前，检测失败的概率被限制在 (4T+1)/p；3. 在(2,4)-树实现中实现了单次操作O(log n)的最坏时间复杂度，且审计记录仅为常数大小。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的审计机制在维持较低的存储成本（仅需极小空间）和高效操作延迟（O(log n)）的同时，能够以极高的概率检测出不可信方对有序集操作的篡改，且无需牺牲审计的正确性。

Abstract: We study retrospective auditing for dynamic ordered sets maintained by an untrusted party. A passive auditor watches insert, delete, membership, predecessor, successor, min, and max operations, stores five machine words and a flag, and receives a constant-size public tally record per operation. At audit time the maintainer discloses the claimed live vacant intervals. The method represents order semantics by maximal gaps: gaps are born, cited, consumed, and timestamped, while two hidden field accumulators test equality of the birth and consumption ledgers. Honest executions are accepted with probability one. If any answer in a T-operation session is wrong, acceptance occurs with probability at most (4T+1)/p over one secret field element, against computationally unbounded maintainers. We prove that deterministic and visible-coin auditors require linear state, and that removing the timestamp rule permits an exact replay forgery. A leaf-oriented (2,4)-tree implements the maintainer in O(log n) worst-case time per operation with one extra word per element, and its rebalancing events admit an auditable O(m) envelope over m updates. Checkpoint audits compose with additive error.

</details>


### [237] [Testing Bipartiteness in Logarithmic Rounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13583)
*Yumou Fei,Ronitt Rubinfeld*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文改进了有界度图二分性的测试复杂度，通过引入 Max-Cut 的半定规划松弛技巧，将随机游走次数优化至 $O(\sqrt{n})$，并给出了最优的流式算法参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进 Goldreich 和 Ron 在有界度图二分性测试中 $O(\sqrt{n \log n})$ 的游走次数及 $O(\log^6 n)$ 的游走长度限制。

Method: 摒弃了传统的路径分析方法，转而采用 Goemans 和 Williamson 提出的 Max-Cut 半定规划（SDP）松弛技术来分析图的性质。

Result: 实现了 $O(\sqrt{n})$ 的随机游走次数和 $O(\log n)$ 的游走长度；并得到了最优的 $O(\log n)$ 趟和 $O(\sqrt{n} \log n)$ 空间的流式算法。

Conclusion: 本文将二分图性质测试的游走次数优化至 $O(\sqrt{n})$，游走长度缩短至 $O(\log n)$，并推导出一个最优的流式算法，证明了基于半定规划（SDP）的新视角在图性质测试中的有效性。

Abstract: The seminal work of Goldreich and Ron (\textit{Combinatorica, 1999}) showed that bipartiteness of bounded-degree graphs can be tested using $O(\sqrt{n\log n})$ random walks of length $O(\log^{6} n)$. In this work, we improve their result by showing that $O(\sqrt{n})$ random walks of length $O(\log n)$ suffice. As a corollary, we obtain an $O(\log n)$-pass, $O(\sqrt{n}\log n)$-space streaming algorithm for testing bipartiteness, whose pass complexity is optimal in light of a recent lower bound of Fei, Minzer, and Wang (\textit{arXiv, 2026}).
  Our proof takes a different approach from that of Goldreich and Ron, using the semidefinite programming relaxation for Max-Cut introduced by Goemans and Williamson (\textit{J. ACM, 1995}).

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [238] [Quickest Detection of Hallucination Onset: Delay Bounds and Learned CUSUM Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12476)
*Igor Itkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将大模型幻觉检测从单纯的分类任务转变为“最快变化检测”问题，研究发现现有评估指标忽略了检测延迟，并利用序贯分析揭示了现有模型在信息提取效率上的本质差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的幻觉检测器多以分类器身份通过AUC指标评估，忽视了流式监控中至关重要的“反应时间”（即检测延迟），未能反映检测器在幻觉发生后的实时响应效率。

Method: 1. 将幻觉检测建模为“最快变化检测（Quickest Change Detection）”问题；2. 基于RAGTruth验证了一阶马尔可夫模型；3. 将因果循环标注器作为具有学习增量的CUSUM算法使用；4. 使用Donsker-Varadhan型信息速率最优性定理分析差距。

Result: 在假阳性率为0.01的情况下，理论检测延迟下界为1.3个token；所提出的递归标注器检测延迟为11-13个token，优于线性基线的31个token；分析显示，目前的学习评分仅实现了特征潜含散度（divergence）的1/4.5。

Conclusion: 分类指标掩盖了幻觉检测的延迟结构，而使用序贯分析（Sequential Analysis）可以使这一延迟可测。研究表明，现有的学习评分方法仍有很大的提升空间。

Abstract: Token-level hallucination detectors are evaluated as classifiers, by AUC over all tokens, yet a streaming monitor is judged by its reaction time: the number of tokens that pass between the onset of a hallucination and the alarm. We formulate hallucination onset detection as a quickest change detection problem. A first-order Markov model of the latent faithful/hallucinated state, validated on RAGTruth, places the task inside classical change-point theory and yields Lorden's lower bound on detection delay: about 1.3 tokens at a false-alarm rate of 0.01. We then show that a causal recurrent labeler acts as a CUSUM with a learned increment; at a matched false-alarm rate it detects in 11-13 tokens, against 31 for a linear per-token baseline, and a controlled decomposition attributes most of this advantage to a better per-token score rather than to temporal accumulation. An information-rate optimality theorem of Donsker-Varadhan type explains the remaining order-of-magnitude gap: the learned score realizes only 1/4.5 of the divergence the features carry, a deficit that recalibration cannot remove, with the remainder a finite-horizon effect. Classification metrics conceal this delay structure; sequential analysis makes it measurable

</details>


### [239] [Boltzmann Attention: Learnable Ising Couplings for Cooperative Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12478)
*Gilhan Kim,Daniel K. Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于 Ising 模型的 Boltzmann Attention 机制，通过引入可学习的成对位置交互，增强了 Transformer 对序列内部依赖的建模能力，并探索了利用量子退火进行高效训练的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准注意力机制在计算相关性时主要依赖 Query-Key 的点积相似度，Softmax 归一化仅引入了隐式的竟争关系，缺乏对序列内部位置间显式交互（如合作或拮抗关系）的参数化建模能力。因此，研究旨在突破这一限制，提升模型对长序列依赖的捕捉能力。

Method: 提出 Boltzmann Attention，一种基于能量模型的注意力机制。该方法在传统数据驱动的局部场基础上，额外引入了可学习的两两耦合参数（pairwise couplings），用于显式建模位置间的关联（相互作用）。此外，引入绝热/非绝热量子退火（diabatic quantum annealing）作为一种实用的训练与采样策略。

Result: 在字符级语言建模及合成括号匹配任务上，Boltzmann Attention 均优于传统的 Softmax Attention，且随着序列长度增加优势愈发明显。消融实验证实了性能提升源于可学习的两两耦合项。同时证明，非绝热量子退火能够在保持计算竞争力的前提下作为一种有效的训练手段。

Conclusion: 显式的序列位置间交互建模是提升注意力机制性能的有效途径。Boltzmann Attention 通过引入 Ising 模型中的两两耦合项，克服了标准 Softmax 注意力对位置独立性的限制，且其物理模型结构为量子计算辅助采样提供了潜在路径。

Abstract: Attention mechanisms are central to modern sequence models, yet standard attention computes relevance primarily through individual query--key similarities. Although softmax normalization introduces competition among positions, a standard attention layer does not explicitly parameterize learnable interactions between attention decisions. This limits its ability to directly model cooperative or antagonistic co-attention structure within the attention mechanism itself. We propose Boltzmann attention, an energy-based generalization in which attention patterns are governed by an interacting Ising model. The method augments the usual data-dependent local fields with learnable pairwise couplings, allowing the model to represent inter-position correlations beyond those captured by softmax or sigmoid attention. Experiments on character-level language modeling and synthetic bracket matching show that Boltzmann attention consistently improves over standard softmax attention within a standard Transformer architecture, with the advantage becoming more pronounced as sequence length increases. A four-way ablation confirms that the improvement arises from the learnable pairwise couplings. These results suggest that explicit inter-position interactions provide a principled enhancement for attention-based sequence modeling. Moreover, the Ising formulation opens a natural path toward quantum-computing-based sampling strategies: we demonstrate that diabatic quantum annealing provides a practical training method while maintaining competitive performance with exact Boltzmann computation.

</details>


### [240] [ReCal: Reward Calibration for RL-based LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12479)
*Qihang Yu,Hanwen Tong,Zhengqi Zhang,Bo Zheng,Feng Wei,Shengyu Zhang,Zemin Liu,Fei Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为解决强化学习驱动的 LLM 路由中奖励分配模糊与训练偏差问题，本文提出了 ReCal 框架，通过分层奖励分解和分布感知优化显著提升了路由效果与训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的 LLM 路由方法在异构任务中存在局限：多目标奖励聚合导致的信用分配模糊，以及不同实例间奖励变异性带来的优化偏差（即偏好简单样本而非信息量大的复杂样本）。

Method: 提出 ReCal 框架：1. 采用分层奖励分解机制（Hierarchical Reward Decomposition），实现组件级别的优势估计；2. 采用分布感知优化策略（Distribution-aware Optimization），通过方差感知的重加权（Variance-aware Reweighted）和数据集级归一化来平衡训练偏差。

Result: 在七个数据集上的实验表明，ReCal 在路由性能和训练稳定性方面均优于现有基线模型。

Conclusion: ReCal 通过奖励分解与分布感知优化，有效缓解了现有路由模型在异构任务中面临的奖励模糊与优化偏差问题，显著提升了 LLM 路由的性能和训练稳定性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) routing has emerged as an effective paradigm for leveraging the complementary strengths of multiple LLMs through dynamic model and reasoning-strategy selection. Recent reinforcement learning (RL)-based routing methods further improve routing quality by optimizing routing policies from interaction feedback. However, they still struggle to provide informative and comparable learning signals under heterogeneous tasks with varying difficulty. In practice, multiple objectives (e.g., correctness, format behavior) are aggregated into a single scalar reward, leading to ambiguous credit assignment and conflicting optimization signals. Moreover, reward signals exhibit significant variability across instances, where some instances produce higher or more variable rewards, introducing optimization bias that favors trivial samples over informative ones. To address these issues, we propose \textbf{ReCal}, a \textbf{\underline{Re}}ward \textbf{\underline{Cal}}ibration framework for RL-based LLM routing. We first introduce a hierarchical reward decomposition mechanism with component-wise advantage estimation. We further propose a distribution-aware optimization strategy that calibrates optimization variability through variance-aware reweighting and per-dataset normalization. Experiments on seven datasets demonstrate that ReCal consistently improves routing performance, and training stability over baselines. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ReCal.

</details>


### [241] [Representing Time Series as Structured Programs for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12481)
*Jaeho Kim,Changhun Oh,Seokhyun Lee,Irina Rish,Changhee Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为了解决 LLM 处理时间序列时的模态差异，T2SP 通过将时序数据转化为结构化符号程序，在无需训练的情况下显著提升了 LLM 对时间序列的分析与推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 处理时间序列时存在模态不匹配（Modality Mismatch）问题，导致模型在处理长序列时性能下降且计算开销巨大。研究旨在寻求一种更优的方法，让 LLM 无需复杂微调即可有效推理时间序列。

Method: 提出 Time-Series-to-Structured-Program (T2SP) 方法。该方法通过确定性的规则将时间序列分解为趋势、周期和突显事件，并将其转化为结构化的符号编程形式，而非传统的原始数值序列序列化。

Result: 在编辑、描述和问答三项推理任务中，T2SP 相比于原始字符串表示法，在性能表现、推理速度和故障率方面均有显著优化。

Conclusion: T2SP 是一种无需训练的有效接口，能够将时间序列无缝转化为 LLM 可理解的编程语言形式，从而增强模型在时间序列分析任务中的推理能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong reasoning and instruction-following capabilities, making them potentially powerful tools for time-series analysis. However, time series lie outside their native textual modality, raising a fundamental question: how should time series be represented so that LLMs can reason about them effectively? Existing work typically serializes raw numerical sequences or fine-tunes pre-trained LLMs on time-series data. These approaches place the burden of extracting temporal structure directly on the LLM, creating a modality mismatch that often degrades performance on long sequences and introduces substantial computational overhead. In this work, we introduce Time-Series-to-Structured-Program representation (T2SP), a deterministic, training-free method that represents a time series as a structured symbolic program. T2SP decomposes time series into trends, periods, and salient events, expressing them in a program-friendly format aligned with the textual and code-like modalities on which LLMs are natively trained. By shifting temporal-structure extraction from the model to the representation itself, T2SP enables off-the-shelf LLMs to leverage their existing reasoning capabilities for time-series understanding. We evaluate T2SP on three reasoning tasks -- editing, captioning, and question answering -- where it consistently improves performance, reduces reasoning time, and lowers failure rates compared with raw-string representations. Our results demonstrate that T2SP provides an effective interface between time series and LLMs.

</details>


### [242] [A Two-Parameter Weibull Framework for Diagnosing Transformer Weight Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18898)
*Tiexin Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出使用 Weibull 分布参数（k, λ）定量诊断 Transformer 权重的演化规律，发现权重的功能类别和训练进度可通过这两个参数实现架构无关的精确刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的聚合统计方法无法刻画 Transformer 内部权重演化的细节。作者旨在建立一种架构无关的度量标准，以揭示权重分布在训练过程中的动力学行为。

Method: 利用 Weibull 分布作为权重幅值（|w|）的诊断框架，对 Transformer 模型中各层权重矩阵进行逐元素拟合，通过形状参数 k 和尺度参数 λ 进行量化分析。实验覆盖 7 个架构家族、12 个模型。

Result: 1. 传输类模块（FFN, W_o）的 k 值极度稳定（约 1.20）；2. 选择类模块（W_q, W_k）的 k 值受到存储方式（GQA vs 分离存储）的显著影响；3. λ 值在训练期间显著增加，并与训练超参数（学习率和权重衰减）呈特定比例关系。

Conclusion: Weibull 参数（k 和 λ）为 Transformer 的训练动力学提供了统一的诊断框架，k 反映了功能类别（与权重架构相关），λ 反映了训练进度，且二者携带独立信息。

Abstract: We apply the Weibull distribution -- a two-parameter family from extreme-value theory -- as a diagnostic framework for element-wise weight magnitude distributions in transformers. At initialization, i.i.d. Gaussian weights give |w| ~ HalfNormal, yielding k ~ 1.20 via middle-80% probability-plot fit (the protocol used throughout this work). This anchor makes k a principled, architecture-independent measuring stick for training dynamics; fitting each weight matrix independently at every layer at every checkpoint enables per-component, per-layer, and per-step diagnostics that aggregate statistics cannot resolve.
  Applying this framework to 12 model entries spanning 7 architectural families (Pythia, OLMo-1/2, LLaMA-3, Mistral, Qwen2.5/3) reveals three findings. First, FFN modules and the attention output projection W_o -- the Transmission Class -- fall in a narrow k band: median terminal k in [1.186, 1.204] across 12 entries (cross-family CV = 0.51%), shared across SwiGLU/GeLU activations, Pre-LN/QK-Norm placements, and 70M-14B sizes. Second, the attention input projections W_q, W_k -- the Selection Class -- depart from the Weibull family, with severity shaped by storage: separately-stored Q/K (OLMo-1, OLMo-2) yields k in [0.76, 0.99] (deep); GQA models yield k in [1.10, 1.16] (mild); Pythia's merged W_qkv occupies a transitional zone tracking training budget T/tau monotonically. Third, lambda grows substantially during training and scales with sqrt(eta/lambda_wd) within the Pythia family (Pearson r = 0.94, three Transmission kinds), directionally consistent with Fan et al. (2025). The two parameters carry independent information: k labels the functional class, lambda labels training progress.
  We release npm-weibull-py v0.4 (Python library) and DATABASE_v9_1 at https://github.com/tiexinding/NPM-Weibull-public .

</details>


### [243] [Scalable anomaly detection via a univariate Christoffel function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12483)
*Florian Grivet,Didier Henrion,Jean-Bernard Lasserre,Louise Travé-Massuyès*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为了解决传统克里斯托费尔函数异常检测方法在高维数据中计算不可行的缺陷，本文提出了 UCF 模型，通过简化计算逻辑消除维度限制，不仅保证了理论优越性，更在实验中证明了远超现有主流算法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于克里斯托费尔函数的异常检测方法虽具有坚实的数学基础，但受限于计算高维矩阵求逆的需求，无法处理中高维数据集，导致实际应用受限。

Method: 提出了一种变体方法 UCF（Univariate Christoffel Function），其核心思想是利用查询点与支持点之间的平方距离，将高维计算简化为一元克里斯托费尔函数计算，从而规避了传统方法中矩阵求逆的指数级复杂度问题。

Result: 在 ADBench 基准测试中，UCF 方法在平均精度（Average Precision）指标上一致地超越了 14 种最先进的基准算法，同时保持了原始方法良好的异常检测特性（如 on-off 支持二分行为和对支持形状的精确捕捉）。

Conclusion: 通过引入 UCF，成功克服了基于克里斯托费尔函数（Christoffel function）方法的维度灾难，提供了一种理论基础稳固、计算高效且在多种基准测试中表现优异的通用异常检测解决方案。

Abstract: Anomaly detection plays a critical role in identifying unusual patterns across domains such as fraud detection, network intrusion, and system fault diagnosis. Recently, Christoffel function-based methods, rooted in polynomial optimization, have emerged as promising alternatives to deep learning due to their strong mathematical foundations and computational frugality. However, their practical applicability is hindered by the need to invert a matrix whose size grows exponentially with the data dimension, rendering the method intractable even for moderate-dimensional datasets. This paper addresses the dimensionality limitations of Christoffel function-based anomaly detection while preserving its key theoretical properties, i.e., the on-off support dichotomy behavior and the accurate support shape capture. We introduce UCF, a univariate Christoffel function which is based on the squared distance between the query point and the support points. Extensive experiments on the ADBench benchmark demonstrate that UCF consistently outperforms 14 state-of-the-art baselines in terms of Average Precision. By resolving the scalability bottleneck of the Christoffel Function, this work expands the toolkit of anomaly detection methods with a robust, theoretically grounded, and universally applicable approach.

</details>


### [244] [Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Chemotherapy Pharmacokinetics: Benchmarking the Clinical Estimator and Exposing Parameter Identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12658)
*Riya Bisht,Dhruv Agarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过化疗药代动力学实例验证了PINN在处理部分观测生物学系统中的价值，证明了PINN在保持传统拟合精度的同时，能通过物理约束揭示复杂系统的可辨识性并适应异构数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生物学的部分观测问题（如药代动力学中难以测量组织药物浓度）中，传统方法（NLS）在复杂或错误设定的模型下失效，而纯数据驱动方法（MLP）缺乏物理归纳偏差，导致预测性能较差，因此需要PINN来结合物理规律与数据。

Method: 将PINN应用于生物学的化疗药代动力学（PK）问题，将其与线性两室模型的标准非线性最小二乘法（NLS）及完全数据驱动的MLP进行了基准测试。特别在Michaelis-Menten（非线性消除）扩展问题中，利用PINN处理复杂微分方程并引入稀疏组织测量以提高参数辨识度。

Result: 在线性问题中，PINN的效果与NLS相当且能同时恢复组织浓度曲线（MLP失败）；在非线性Michaelis-Menten问题中，NLS失效，而PINN能通过收敛特性揭示模型不可辨识性，且在引入少量稀疏组织测量后显著提高了参数恢复精度。

Conclusion: PINNs提供了一种统一的框架，能够处理标准模型无法解决的非线性/复杂动力学问题，并能有效识别模型的参数可辨识性，同时通过灵活的损失函数设计融合异构数据。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) are an attractive tool for partial-observation problems in biology, where the governing dynamics are known but some compartments cannot be measured. Chemotherapy pharmacokinetics (PK) is a clean instance: drug concentration in plasma is routinely measured, but concentration in tissue -- which determines tumour kill and off-target toxicity -- is not. We benchmark a PINN against the standard clinical baseline (nonlinear least-squares on the analytical biexponential plasma solution, hereafter NLS) and a physics-agnostic neural baseline (a data-only MLP) on two PK problems. On the linear two-compartment problem, NLS is near-optimal; the PINN matches it to within a small constant factor while also producing the tissue curve in a single training pass, whereas the data-only MLP fails on tissue by roughly 10x. On a Michaelis-Menten extension (saturable elimination), the biexponential closed form no longer exists, so NLS is mis-specified and silently returns meaningless rate constants. The PINN instead exposes a deeper fact: the Michaelis-Menten two-compartment model is non-identifiable from plasma alone, and the PINN reports this honestly by converging to a basin with k12 -> 0. Adding two sparse tissue observations largely resolves identifiability: across five seeds the PINN recovers k21 to within 1% of truth and Vmax, Km to within one standard-deviation bar, while k12 moves in the correct direction (0.02 -> 0.82) but remains ~2 sigma below truth -- a recovery the closed-form NLS estimator cannot attempt at all, because its biexponential ansatz describes only plasma. Our claim is not that PINNs beat NLS. It is that PINNs offer a uniform recipe that ties the textbook estimator on the textbook problem, exposes structural identifiability that
  the textbook estimator hides, and absorbs heterogeneous measurements within a single loss.

</details>


### [245] [Positional Encoding in the Context of Memristor-Based Analog Computation for Automatic Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13379)
*Benedikt Hilmes,Nick Rossenbach,Ralf Schlüter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现忆阻器在进行NLP计算时，位置编码的大输出值会导致ADC失真；通过动态调整位宽分配或移除线性变换，可在维持相同能耗的条件下显著改善计算精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 忆阻器虽能实现高效的向量矩阵乘法（VMM），但在NLP任务中由于权值编程和执行过程的失真，导致模数转换阶段存在显著的性能下降。

Method: 1. 分析并识别出转换后位置编码产生的大输出值是导致ADC失真的主要原因；2. 提出两种优化方法：一是调整特定忆阻器层的权值与ADC精度位匹配比例，二是移除编码相关的线性变换。

Result: 在调整ADC位宽匹配后，执行性能下降相对减少约50%；在无法修改ADC的情况下，移除特定线性转换可将性能下降相对减少约30%，且均保持了能耗稳定。

Conclusion: 通过优化量化策略（调整位宽分配）或移除特定的线性变换，可以显著缓解模数转换器（ADC）带来的精度损失，从而在不增加功耗的前提下提升忆阻器执行NLP计算的性能。

Abstract: Memristors provide a new chance for resource-efficient computation of neural models for natural language processing by enabling analog execution of vector-matrix-multiplication. Yet, computations on these devices are currently subject to larger distortion, both in weight programming and execution. In this work, we identify large output values of transformed positional encodings to cause major degradation within analog-to-digital conversion (ADC) as part of memristor-based computation. By adjusting the proportion of weight and precision bits of the ADC of specific memristor layers, we reduce the degradation of the execution by ~50% relative, while keeping the estimated energy consumption stable. Additionally, we investigate scenarios where the ADC cannot be modified. In that case the degradation can be reduced by ~30% relative after removing encoding-related linear transformations.

</details>


### [246] [Speculative Rollback Correction for Quality-Diverse Web Agent Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12485)
*Longkun Hao,Hongyu Lin,Hao Li,Zhichao Yang,Haojie Hao,Dongshuo Huang,Haitao Yang,Hongyu Ge,Ming jie Xie,Yanjun Wu,Zi Hao Yin,Yan Bai,Yihang Lou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 Web 智能体模仿学习中专家介入时机难把控的问题，SRC 提出了一种基于固定视界分支评审的框架，通过智能体先行探索、专家按需回滚纠正与轨迹筛选机制，显著提升了模型训练的数据质量与策略鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在交互式 Web 智能体中，通过专家模仿学习进行训练时，确定专家介入时机存在困难：过晚介入会导致无法挽回的状态崩溃，过早或过度介入会导致智能体产生依赖并陷入局部最优。

Method: 提出了 Speculative Rollback Correction (SRC) 模仿学习框架：1. 采用固定视界分支评审，在智能体执行短片段后进行教师评估；2. 仅在检测到偏离时进行回滚（Rollback）纠正；3. 利用硬件验证器过滤成功轨迹并存入质量-多样性存档；4. 基于纠正后的轨迹和验证通过的数据进行监督微调。

Result: 在 WebArena-Infinity 基准测试中，SRC 成功收集了 977 条高质量验证通过轨迹及 9,183 个下一步操作示例，在恢复效率与专家查询开销之间达到了更优的平衡。

Conclusion: SRC 框架通过引入固定视界分支评审机制（Fixed-horizon branch review），有效平衡了专家介入频率与智能体自主探索的需求，显著缓解了因过度依赖或介入不及时导致的模型训练失效问题，实现了更高效的数据采集与策略微调。

Abstract: Training interactive web agents through imitation learning from expert trajectories has emerged as a highly effective approach. However, determining the optimal timing for expert intervention presents a critical challenge in this context. Delayed intervention often leads to the accumulation of early-stage errors, pushing the page state into an irrecoverable regime. Conversely, premature or excessive intervention causes the agent to become overly reliant on expert policies, trapping the model in local optima characterized by a single, rigid trajectory. We propose Speculative Rollback Correction (SRC), a branch-level imitation framework for resettable agent environments. Instead of requesting teacher labels at every visited state or correcting only after a completed trajectory, SRC uses fixed-horizon branch review: the student executes a short speculative segment before teacher review, and the teacher localizes the first harmful deviation only when local progress breaks. Rollback preserves useful prefixes, while successful rollouts are filtered by a hard verifier and retained in a lightweight quality-diversity archive. The resulting data supports next-action supervised fine-tuning on both localized corrections and verifier-passing trajectories. On WebArena-Infinity, SRC collects 977 verifier-passing trajectories and 9,183 next-action examples; fixed-horizon review improves the recovery-versus-query tradeoff over step-level review while retaining verifier-passing solution variants. Code is available at https://github.com/LongkunHao/SRC_gui_agent.

</details>


### [247] [How Useful is Causal Invariance for Domain Adaptation in Finite-Sample Settings?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12680)
*Julia Kostin,Kasra Jalaldoust,Elias Bareinboim,Samory Kpotufe,Fanny Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究探讨了在监督领域自适应（sDA）中利用因果知识的有效性。理论分析表明，只有当基于因果不变性构建的候选预测器之间存在足够的目标风险余量时，因果知识才能在有限样本下提升性能，否则可能导致无法避免的负迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的因果领域泛化（DG）研究主要集中在群体层面的不变性，但缺乏对有限样本设置（特别是监督领域自适应 sDA）中，利用因果知识是否能真正提升模型性能及其适用范围的系统研究。

Method: 1. 在线性回归设置下，基于因果知识指定多个不变或部分不变特征子集。
2. 推导证明了有限样本下的匹配上下界（Upper and Lower Bounds），揭示了性能增益受候选预测器间的目标风险余量制约。
3. 提出一种自适应聚合程序。
4. 在线性结构因果模型（SCM）中理论分析结构偏移的影响，并利用真实因果基准数据集进行验证。

Result: 1. 给出了在何种条件下因果知识能够保证提升 sDA 性能的理论判定准则（即风险余量需足够大）。
2. 开发了一种能够匹配最佳候选预测器，且在目标样本有限时能避免负迁移的算法。
3. 揭示了预测性能增益与线性 SCM 中结构偏移大小的直接量化关系。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在监督领域自适应（sDA）中，因果结构知识是否能带来有限样本增益，取决于候选预测器之间的风险余量（Risk Margins）与有限源样本估计误差之间的权衡。当余量足够大时，自适应聚合能有效避免负迁移；否则，无法利用因果知识实现更优的收敛速度。

Abstract: Machine learning models often degrade when they are deployed on a target distribution that differs from the source distributions they were trained on. Recent work in causality-based domain generalization has shown how shared causal structure between domains can induce invariant predictors, e.g., models on a subset of features which have stable risk across structured domain shifts. However, the extent to which such population-level causal invariances can lead to gains in finite-sample settings remains underexplored. In particular, in practice we often have access to a few labeled target samples, a setting called supervised domain adaptation (sDA). In this paper, we explore when (full or partial) causal knowledge can provably improve supervised domain adaptation.
  As a first step, we study linear regression, where full or partial causal knowledge specifies a collection of invariant or possibly invariant feature subsets, each yielding a source-trained candidate predictor. We derive matching upper and lower bounds showing that finite-sample gains are governed by the target-risk margins separating the candidates, together with the finite-source estimation error. When these margins are sufficiently large relative to $n_Q$, an adaptive aggregation procedure can match the best candidate predictor while avoiding negative transfer relative to target-only learning. On the other hand, when the margins are too small, no algorithm can reliably exploit the candidate collection to obtain faster finite-sample rates. We further connect these margins to structural shift magnitude in linear SCMs and validate the theory on real-world causal benchmarks.

</details>


### [248] [An Empirical Study on Predictive Maintenance for Component X in Heavy-Duty Scania Trucks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12486)
*Valeriu Dimidov,Sasan Jafarnejad,Raphaël Frank*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种简化卡车PdM的方法，通过将传感器数据转化为单调非递减的时间序列表格，配合AutoML模型，在Scania数据集上实现了比现有技术更低的成本和更简单的建模流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卡车车队实施基于状态的预测性维护（PdM）虽能降低停机成本，但面临海量数据处理、故障检测复杂性以及部署成本权衡等挑战，亟需一种高效且易于实现的维护策略。

Method: 提出了一种基于单调非递减时间序列假设的预测性维护方法；通过仅选取时间序列中的最新观测数据，将其转换为表格格式，并利用AutoML驱动的机器学习模型进行预测和故障分类。

Result: 在Scania Component X数据集上的实验结果表明，该方法在保持高精度的同时，在成本控制上优于现有的最先进方法，且通过AutoML简化了开发流程。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的基于“单调非递减时间序列”假设的PdM方法，在降低维护成本和简化建模流程方面优于现有最先进技术，验证了AutoML在车辆故障预测中的有效性。

Abstract: Condition-based Predictive Maintenance (PdM) for truck fleets has gained momentum in recent years. This maintenance strategy aims to minimize unplanned downtimes and reduce costs by monitoring the health status of vehicles and taking proactive action based on their condition. However, the implementation of condition-based PdM systems is challenging due to the large volume of data generated by the trucks, the inherent complexity of detecting failures through sensor data and the difficulties in finding cost-effective trade-offs in the solution's implementation. In this paper, we define and validate a condition-based PdM methodology built on the assumption that the wear-and-tear state of the monitored component can be represented as a monotonically non-decreasing time series. It involves selecting only the most recent observations from the time series and transforming them into a tabular format for classification using machine learning (ML) models designed for tabular data. Our results indicate that the proposed methodology reduces costs on the Scania Component X dataset compared to current state-of-the-art (SOTA) approaches, while also simplifying the modeling process through AutoML.

</details>


### [249] [Two-Layer Linear Auto-Regressive Models Estimate Latent States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12691)
*Yahya Sattar,Sunmook Choi,Leo Maynard-Zhang,Yassir Jedra,Maryam Fazel,Sarah Dean*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过理论分析证明，双层线性自回归模型通过经验风险最小化训练后，能够自动收敛到卡尔曼滤波的解，成功从序列数据中隐式恢复出底层动力系统的状态表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的自回归模型在处理序列数据时表现优异，但其学习潜在表示的内在机制尚不明确，需要从理论上解释为何这些模型能够捕捉潜在的状态动力学。

Method: 1. 证明线性广义系统可以通过有限截断的自回归模型进行有效近似；2. 分析双层线性模型的优化景观，证明其非凸优化任务中不存在恶劣的局部极小值（即所有驻点均为全局最优或鞍点）；3. 提供关于预测误差、参数估计误差和潜在状态恢复的有限样本理论保证。

Result: 理论推导和数值实验均表明，自回归模型不仅能预测序列，还能有效重构底层线性系统的状态估计，且该训练过程具备良好的收敛性质。

Conclusion: 即使在缺乏动态模型先验知识的情况下，经过训练的双层线性自回归模型可以通过梯度下降隐式地学习到卡尔曼滤波（Kalman filter），其学到的潜在表示与最优卡尔曼滤波的状态估计量在相似变换下一致。

Abstract: Auto-regressive models have emerged as powerful tools for sequential data, from language to video. Understanding how and why these models learn latent representations remains an open theoretical question. In this work, we demonstrate that when trained by empirical risk minimization on data from partially observed linear dynamical systems, two-layer linear auto-regressive models naturally learn to approximate Kalman filtering. In particular, we show that the learned hidden representation coincides, up to a similarity transformation, with the state estimates produced by the optimal (Kalman) filter, even though the model has no explicit knowledge of the underlying dynamics or state. The result follows from three main insights. First, we establish that the Kalman filter is well approximated by an auto-regressive model with bounded truncation error. Second, we show that despite non-convexity, the two-layer optimization landscape is benign, i.e., all stationary points are either strict saddles or global minima. Finally, as our main contributions, we provide finite-sample guarantees on prediction error, parameter estimation error, and latent state recovery. Numerical simulations support the theoretical results and demonstrate that the latent representations of auto-regressive models recover state estimates.

</details>


### [250] [DynamicPTQ: Mitigating Activation Quantization Collapse via Residual-Stream Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12487)
*Zimo Zhao,Maolin Wang,Bowen Yu,Bowen Liu,Xiao Han,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 4-bit 大模型量化中激活值不稳定问题，本文提出了 DynamicPTQ。它通过分析残差流的相位特征，识别敏感层并采用混合精度（8-bit/4-bit）量化，在提升模型性能的同时显著提高了推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 W4A4KV4（权重、激活、KV 缓存均为 4-bit）极端量化场景下，激活中的异常值（massive activations）会导致严重的量化误差。现有的静态平滑方法忽略了跨层的残差流动态，无法解决该阶段性的不稳定问题。

Method: 1. 提出 Jump Ratio 和 Historical Feature SNR 指标来定量分析残差流中的跨层动态特性。
2. 提出 DynamicPTQ（一种相位感知混合精度量化策略），根据残差流动力学识别对量化敏感的层。
3. 动态将敏感层激活设为 8-bit，其余部分保持 4-bit（W4A4KV4）。
4. 可与现有 PTQ 基准（如 QuaRot, SpinQuant, FlatQuant）无缝集成。

Result: 在 LLaMA-2 和 LLaMA-3 上实验显示，DynamicPTQ 显著改进了困惑度（Perplexity）和零样本问答（Zero-shot QA）性能，在保持低内存开销的同时，实现了 1.05 到 1.07 倍的推理吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: DynamicPTQ 通过分析残差流动力学，引入相位感知混合精度量化策略，验证了在 W4A4KV4 设置下，仅对敏感层使用 8-bit 量化即可有效解决量化不稳定性，提升了大模型推理的性能与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is essential for efficient large language model inference, but reliably quantizing activations remains challenging when weights, activations, and KV caches are all quantized to 4-bit precision. A key difficulty lies in massive activations, whose extreme values dominate the activation range and amplify quantization errors. State-of-the-art methods mainly mitigate massive activations through transformation-based smoothing, such as orthogonal rotations and affine scaling, but overlook the cross-layer dynamics of the residual stream. In this paper, we show that massive activations emerge and disappear in a phase-wise pattern across network depth, triggering large residual changes. These changes cause newly injected layer-wise updates to dominate the 4-bit quantization scale and weaken historical residual information. To characterize this behavior, we introduce Jump Ratio and Historical Feature SNR. This suggests that static transformation-based smoothing cannot fully resolve dynamic quantization instability caused by cross-layer residual changes. Based on this analysis, we propose DynamicPTQ, a Dynamic Post-Training Quantization policy for phase-aware mixed-precision activation quantization. DynamicPTQ identifies quantization-sensitive layers from residual-stream dynamics and assigns 8-bit activation precision only to these layers, while keeping weights, KV caches, and other activations in 4-bit precision. It can be directly integrated with strong PTQ baselines such as QuaRot, SpinQuant, and FlatQuant. Experiments on LLaMA-2 and LLaMA-3 show that DynamicPTQ consistently improves perplexity and zero-shot QA performance under W4A4KV4 quantization, while achieving 1.05 to 1.07 times throughput improvement with modest memory overhead. These results demonstrate a practical path toward robust low-bit LLM inference.

</details>


### [251] [A Stationary (and Therefore Compatible) Representation is All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12488)
*Niccolò Biondi,Federico Pernici,Simone Ricci,Alberto Del Bimbo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用d-Simplex固定分类器结合对比损失训练，在模型频繁更新和替换的场景下，实现了特征的向前/向后兼容，有效避免了检索库的重新索引并提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在模型更新与升级期间，模型特征表示通常会发生改变，导致原有的索引（如检索库中的图像特征）失效。该研究旨在通过学习“兼容性表示（Compatible Representations）”，使得更新后的模型能够直接复用旧模型的特征，从而无需昂贵的重新处理成本。

Method: 1. 利用d-Simplex固定分类器进行模型训练，通过交叉熵损失实现一阶统计量对齐。
2. 引入对比损失与交叉熵损失的凸组合，以捕捉表示中的高阶依赖关系。
3. 在模型序列微调及偶尔更换模型架构的场景下验证了该方案的有效性。

Result: 该方法在实现模型兼容的同时，解决了单一交叉熵损失无法捕捉高阶特征依赖的问题。实验证明，该方法在序列微调及模型替换场景下均表现优异，实现了SOTA效果。

Conclusion: 固定d-Simplex分类器能通过固定特征表示实现模型兼容性，维持检索服务的持续性，避免了重新处理已有数据（gallery images），且通过引入对比学习与交叉熵损失的组合，在提升模型性能的同时达到了SOTA水平。

Abstract: Learning compatible representations aims to learn feature representations that can be used interchangeably over time whenever a model undergoes updates. In this paper, we demonstrate that stationary representations learned by d-Simplex fixed classifiers imply compatibility as in its formal definition. This result establishes a foundation for future works and can be directly exploited in practical learning scenarios. We address the challenge of learning compatibility using $d$-Simplex fixed classifiers when the model is sequentially fine-tuned. Learning according to a d-Simplex fixed classifier with the cross-entropy loss aligns feature distributions at the first-order statistics. Consequently, it may not fully capture higher-order dependencies in the representation between model updates. To address this issue, we demonstrate that training the model using a $d$-Simplex fixed classifier through a convex combination of the cross-entropy loss and a contrastive loss not only captures higher-order dependencies, but is also equivalent to learning with the cross-entropy under the compatibility constraints. We confirm our findings with extensive experiments also considering a new scenario where a pre-trained model is sequentially fine-tuned and occasionally replaced with an improved model. We show that stationary representations enable uninterrupted retrieval services (without reprocessing gallery images) while improving performance during model updates and replacements, achieving state-of-the-art. Code at https://github.com/miccunifi/iamcl2r.

</details>


### [252] [Adaptive Weighted Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12763)
*Aditya Bhaskara,Ashok Cutkosky,Ravi Kumar,Manish Purohit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决并分析了仅凭单次无偏估计选择最大值的问题，设计出一种既符合可容许性又能提供“无妥协”性能保障的策略，并将其成功应用于改善随机优化中的在线转批量算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在仅拥有各未知数值 $x_i$ 的无偏估计 $y_i$ 的条件下，旨在解决如何选出最大值的问题。现有的策略往往在最优性和鲁棒性之间存在权衡，作者希望设计出一种既不被其他策略完全压制，又能提供优于简单随机选择的性能保障的策略。

Method: 通过构建数学决策模型，设计了同时满足可容许性（admissibility，即不被其他策略一致性占优）和基准保护（不劣于均匀随机选择）的策略。在随机优化应用中，通过这些策略从迭代序列中选择最优解，实现了优于标准随机取样方法的性能。

Result: 证明了所设计策略的有效性，这些策略在执行随机优化时能提供“无妥协”保证：在最坏情况下性能不弱于标准随机迭代选择，而在良好的场景下表现显著优于随机选择。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一类“无妥协”（no-compromise）的选择策略。这些策略在保证不劣于随机选择基准的前提下，实现了在特定情况下的性能优化，并成功应用于随机优化中的在线转批量（online-to-batch）设置。

Abstract: We study the problem of selecting the largest among $n$ unknown values $x_1,\dots,x_n$ given only a single unbiased estimate $y_i$ for each $x_i$. We design strategies that are simultaneously admissible (not uniformly dominated by any other strategy) and also never worse than a given baseline such as uniform random selection. We provide an application to stochastic optimization, where we obtain online-to-batch conversion bounds with a desirable "no-compromise" guarantee: they are never worse than standard random iterate selection, and yet can be significantly better in benign settings.

</details>


### [253] [Robustness Verification of Recurrent Neural Networks with Abstraction Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12490)
*Li-Jen Lin,Chih-Duo Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对RNN鲁棒性验证中误差随时间累积的问题，提出了一种结合SHAP引导的关键时间步分裂策略的抽象-细化框架，显著提升了验证的精确度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的循环神经网络（RNN）鲁棒性验证方法依赖线性界传播，但其非线性弛豫误差会随循环连接在时间序列上不断累积和放大，导致验证过于保守，无法证明许多实际鲁棒输入的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出了一种抽象-细化（Abstraction-Refinement）验证框架：针对非线性激活函数（ReLU/tanh/sigmoid）引起的误差，通过对越过零点的区间进行分裂（Partitioning）以降低弛豫误差；引入基于SHAP值的策略，识别并优先细化对验证目标贡献最大的关键时间步，以控制计算复杂度。

Result: 在CIFAR10和MNIST数据集上进行的实验表明，该方法比单纯使用抽象的方法在验证成功率和鲁棒边界紧致性方面有显著提升，并揭示了针对ReLU和tanh模型在计算开销上的性能权衡。

Conclusion: 该论文提出的基于SHAP引导的时间步长选择和区间分裂的细化框架，有效解决了RNN鲁棒性验证中非线性弛豫误差随时间积累的问题，在保证计算效率的同时显著提升了验证成功率和鲁棒边界紧致性。

Abstract: Certified local robustness verification for recurrent neural networks (RNNs) is challenging because approximation errors introduced by nonlinear relaxations can propagate through recurrent connections and accumulate over time. As a result, scalable linear bound propagation methods often become overly conservative and fail to certify inputs that are in fact robust, especially when many pre-activation intervals cross zero. We propose an abstraction-refinement framework for RNN verification that partitions such intervals to remove the dominant relaxation error: on each refined branch, ReLU becomes exact, and smooth activations such as tanh and sigmoid admit substantially tighter linear envelopes. To control the combinatorial cost of splitting in long sequences, we introduce a SHAP-guided timestep selection strategy that ranks hidden states by their contribution to the verification objective and refines only the most critical timesteps in temporal order. Experiments on CIFAR10 and MNIST stroke benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements in verification success and robustness-margin tightness over abstraction-only baselines, while exposing clear runtime trade-offs between ReLU and tanh models.

</details>


### [254] [Reliability of Probabilistic Emulation of Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12997)
*Sam F. Greenbury,Radka Jersakova,Paolo Conti,Marjan Famili,Christopher Iliffe Sprague,Edwin Brown,Jason D. McEwen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过构建 AutoCast 框架对比了物理系统概率预测的两种主流方法，研究发现 CRPS 训练的集成模型在保证预测准确性的前提下，相比生成模型具有更好的不确定性可靠性和推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前物理系统概率预测主要有两种主流方案（生成模型 vs. CRPS 集成模型），但两者在不确定性估计的可靠性方面缺乏系统性的实证比较。

Method: 构建了一个名为 AutoCast 的模块化框架，在计算预算和模型规模匹配的情况下，对比了生成模型（扩散/流匹配）与基于 CRPS 损失函数的集成模型在多种 2D 时空物理系统上的表现。评估指标涵盖预测准确性、经验覆盖率（不确定性可靠性）及推理延迟。

Result: 1. CRPS 集成模型在单步及自回归预测中提供了更可靠的置信区间覆盖率；2. CRPS 集成模型的推理速度显著快于生成模型；3. 生成模型需要在 ambient 空间训练才能达到与 CRPS 集成模型相当的覆盖率，但推理延迟高；4. CRPS 集成模型在潜空间使用时不会出现显著的覆盖率下降。

Conclusion: CRPS训练的集成模型在物理系统概率预测中具有更优的不确定性可靠性（覆盖率更好）以及更高的推理效率。虽然生成模型在 ambient 空间中表现尚可，但计算成本极高，且 CRPS 集成模型在潜空间（latent space）下表现更具鲁棒性。

Abstract: Two dominant approaches have emerged for generating probabilistic forecasts of physical systems: generative models, such as diffusion or flow matching; and ensembles of deterministic models with stochasticity injected, trained using the continuous ranked probability score (CRPS) loss. While both approaches have demonstrated strong predictive accuracy, the reliability of their uncertainties has not been systematically assessed. We address this gap by developing a framework to evaluate both approaches across diverse 2D spatiotemporal physical systems, under matched model size and computational budget. We assess the reliability of probabilistic emulation by inspecting the empirical coverage of predictive intervals, while also considering accuracy and computational efficiency metrics. CRPS-trained ensembles typically achieve more reliable uncertainties on both single-step prediction and autoregressive rollouts, demonstrating better coverage than the standard alternative of training generative models in a latent space. Moreover, the CRPS approach offers significantly faster inference. When generative models are trained in ambient rather than a compressed latent space, which is often infeasible for high-dimensional problems, they exhibit comparable coverage to CRPS-trained ensembles, though with substantially larger inference latency. In contrast, when CRPS-trained ensembles are trained in latent space they do not show a marked degradation in coverage with respect to ambient space. Both generative models and CRPS-trained ensembles demonstrate good predictive accuracy. To facilitate future research and application, we release AutoCast, a modular framework implementing both generative models and CRPS-trained ensembles, alongside AutoSim, a flexible dataset generation package for rapid prototyping.

</details>


### [255] [Net-Ev$^2$: A Generative Simulator for Network Event Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12494)
*Guangyu Wang,Zhaonan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Net-Ev$^2$，一种结合图神经网络与扩散模型的生成式框架，旨在精确模拟复杂网络中事件（如事故）的传播演化，成功解决了多模态信息融合与拓扑结构保持的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成式模拟器难以同时处理结构化属性与非结构化语义，且在模拟网络事件演变时无法有效保持网络拓扑结构。

Method: 提出了一种名为 Net-Ev$^2$ 的生成式模拟器，包含两个核心阶段：1) 结构引导的掩码预训练；2) 拓扑感知扩散过程（通过 U-Net 架构的图下采样与上采样实现）。支持仅通过自然语言输入进行推理。

Result: 提出了名为 Net-Ev$^2$-6.5M 的多模态基准数据集和新的拓扑保真度评估指标 JL-MMD。实验表明，该模型在基准测试中达到了最先进（SOTA）的性能。

Conclusion: Net-Ev$^2$ 实现了对网络事件演化的精确模拟，兼顾了结构属性与语义特征，在保持网络拓扑保真度方面优于现有模型，并具备强大的泛化能力。

Abstract: Reducing real-world trial and error has long been a central goal of decision making, and generative simulators advance this goal by modeling the evolution of future states. An even more challenging yet meaningful task is simulating how disturbance events (e.g., accidents) propagate their impacts across real-world networks. The existing approaches fall short of modeling both structured attributes and unstructured semantics of events, and capturing topological structures in simulating network event evolution. Therefore, we are motivated to propose Net-Ev$^2$ ($\underline{\textbf{Net}}$work $\underline{\textbf{Ev}}$ent $\underline{\textbf{Ev}}$olution), a novel generative simulator that jointly leverages event cues while preserving network topology in simulations. Specifically, the framework consists of two stages, namely structure-guided masked pre-training and topology-aware diffusion process, which is achieved by U-Net-like graph downsampling and upsampling during denoising. At inference time, Net-Ev$^2$ can generate simulations using natural-language event input only, with greater flexibility for practical usage. Furthermore, we introduce Net-Ev$^2$-6.5M, a multimodal benchmark of aligned event and network traffic data across four large-scale road networks, as well as a new topology-aware metric, namely JL-MMD, to evaluate topological fidelity in generated network dynamics. Extensive experiments demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance and strong generalization ability of Net-Ev$^2$. Code is made available at https://github.com/Guangyu4/Net-Ev-2.

</details>


### [256] [Towards More General Control of Diffusion Models Using Jeffrey Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13240)
*Raphaël Razafindralambo,Rémy Sun,Frédéric Precioso,Jes Frellsen,Pierre-Alexandre Mattei*

Main category: cs.LG
